````math
if $`\lambda_{\min}(\Lambda_{0})>\max\{1,L^{2}\}`$, the following
inequality holds:

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sum_{s=1}^{T}\min\left\{1,\|\phi_{s}\|_{\Lambda_{s-1}^{-1}}^{2}\right\}\leq 2\log\left(\frac{\det(\Lambda_{T})}{\det(\Lambda_{0})}\right).
``` |  |

###### Lemma A.5 (Lemma F.3 in [Du et al. \[2021\]](#bib.bib72)).

Let $`\mathcal{X}\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}`$ and suppose
$`\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\|x\|_{2}\leq B_{\mathcal{X}}`$. Then for any
$`n\in\mathbb{N}`$, we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\forall\lambda>0:\quad\max_{x_{1},\ldots,x_{n}\in\mathcal{X}}\log\det\left(I_{d}+\frac{1}{\lambda}\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}x_{i}^{\top}\right)\leq d\log\left(1+\frac{nB_{\mathcal{X}}^{2}}{d\lambda}\right).
``` |  |

As a direct consequence of lemmas
[A.4](#A1.Thmlemma4 "Lemma A.4 (Lemma 11 in [ ] ). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and
[A.5](#A1.Thmlemma5 "Lemma A.5 (Lemma F.3 in [ ] ). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
we have

###### Lemma A.6 (Elliptical Potential Lemma).

Let $`\mathbf{x}_{1},\ldots,\mathbf{x}_{T}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}`$ satisfy
$`\|\mathbf{x}_{t}\|_{2}\leq 1`$ for all $`t\in[T]`$. Fix $`\lambda>0`$,
and let
$`V_{t}=\lambda\mathbf{I}+\sum_{i=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{x}_{i}\mathbf{x}_{i}^{\top}`$.
Then

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\min\left\{1,\|\mathbf{x}_{t}\|^{2}_{V_{t}^{-1}}\right\}\leq 2d\log\big(1+\lambda^{-1}T/d\big).
``` |  |

Specifically for $`\lambda=1`$ we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\min\left\{1,\|\mathbf{x}_{t}\|^{2}_{V_{t}^{-1}}\right\}=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{x}_{t}\|^{2}_{V_{t}^{-1}}\leq 2d\log\big(1+T/d\big).
``` |  |

###### Lemma A.7 (Lemma D.1 in [Jin et al. \[2020\]](#bib.bib4)).

Consider the matrix
$`\Sigma_{t}=\lambda\mathbf{I}+\sum_{i=1}^{t-1}\phi_{i}\phi_{i}^{\top},`$
where $`\phi_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}`$ and $`\lambda>0`$. Then the
following inequality holds $`\forall\;t`$:

|     |                                                               |     |
|-----|---------------------------------------------------------------|-----|
|     |
       ``` math
       \sum_{i=1}^{t-1}\phi_{i}^{\top}\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\phi_{i}\leq d.
       ```                                                            |     |

###### Lemma A.8 (Lemma D.4 in [Jin et al. \[2020\]](#bib.bib4)).

Consider a stochastic process $`\{s_{\tau}\}_{\tau=1}^{\infty}`$ on a
state space $`\mathcal{S}`$ with associated filtration
$`\{\mathcal{F}_{\tau}\}_{\tau=0}^{\infty}`$, and an
$`\mathbb{R}^{d}`$-valued process $`\{\phi_{\tau}\}_{\tau=0}^{\infty}`$
such that $`\phi_{\tau}\in\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}`$ and
$`\|\phi_{\tau}\|\leq 1`$. Define
$`\Lambda_{k}=\lambda\mathbf{I}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{k}\phi_{\tau}\phi_{\tau}^{\top}.`$
Let $`\mathcal{V}`$ be a function class such that
$`\sup_{x}|V(x)|\leq B_{1}`$ for some constant $`B_{1}>0`$, and let
$`\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}`$ be its $`\epsilon`$-covering number under the
distance
$`\operatorname{dist}(V_{1},V_{2})=\sup_{s}|V_{1}(s)-V_{2}(s)|`$. Then,
for any $`\delta\in(0,1)`$, with probability at least $`1-\delta`$, for
all $`k\geq 0`$ and any $`V\in\mathcal{V}`$, we have:

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\left\|\sum_{\tau=1}^{k}\phi_{\tau}\big\{V(s_{\tau})-\mathbb{E}[V(s_{\tau})|\mathcal{F}_{\tau-1}]\big\}\right\|_{\Lambda_{k}^{-1}}^{2}\leq 4B_{1}^{2}\left[\frac{d}{2}\log\left(\frac{k+\lambda}{\lambda}\right)+\log\left(\frac{\mathcal{N}_{\epsilon}}{\delta}\right)\right]+\frac{8k^{2}\epsilon^{2}}{\lambda}.
``` |  |

## Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs

###### Proposition B.1 (Optimism/Concentration).

Let $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$ be the event
$`\|\overline{\omega}_{t}-\omega^{\star}\|_{\Sigma_{t}}\leq\eta_{T}`$,
then we have $`\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{1})\geq(1-\delta/3)`$, under the
event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$ we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle|(\overline{A}_{t}(i,j)-A(i,j))|`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq b_{t}(i,j)\quad\forall(i,j),`$ |  | (34a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle A^{+}_{t}(i,j)-A(i,j)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq 2b_{t}(i,j)\quad\text{and}\quad A^{+}_{t}(i,j)\geq A(i,j)\quad\forall(i,j),`$ |  | (34b) |
|  | $`\displaystyle A(i,j)-A_{t}^{-}(i,j)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq 2b_{t}(i,j)\quad\text{and}\quad A(i,j)\geq A_{t}^{-}(i,j)\quad\forall(i,j),`$ |  | (34c) |

where $`b_{t}(i,j)=\eta_{T}\|\phi(i,j)\|_{\Sigma_{t}^{-1}}`$ and
$`\eta_{T}=\sigma\sqrt{d\log\left(\frac{3(1+2T/\lambda)}{\delta}\right)}+\sqrt{\lambda d}`$.

Proof. Recall that $`\overline{\omega}_{t}`$ is computed in Algorithm
[1](#alg1 "Algorithm 1 ‣ Goal: Regret Minimization. ‣ 2.1 Problem Setup ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
as

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{\omega}_{t}=\arg\min_{\omega\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sum_{(i,j,\widehat{A}(i,j))\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\left(A_{\omega}(i,j)-\widehat{A}(i,j)\right)^{2}+\lambda\|\omega\|_{2}^{2}.
``` |  |

Now using Lemma
[A.3](#A1.Thmlemma3 "Lemma A.3 (Confidence Ellipsoid: Theorem 2 [ ] ). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
with $`S=d`$, $`L=1`$ (cf. Assumption
[1](#Thmassumption1 "Assumption 1 (Linear function approximation ( , )). ‣ 2.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and accounting for the $`2(t-1)`$ points collected until $`t`$, we have
$`\forall\;t\geq 0`$

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\|\overline{\omega}_{t}-\omega^{\star}\|_{\Sigma_{t}}\leq\sigma\sqrt{d\log\left(\frac{3(1+2t/\lambda)}{\delta}\right)}+\sqrt{\lambda d}\quad\text{w.p.}\;1-\delta/3.`$ |  | (35) |

Since
$`\eta_{T}=\sigma\sqrt{d\log\left(\frac{3(1+2T/\lambda)}{\delta}\right)}+\sqrt{\lambda d}`$
we have $`\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{1})=1-\delta/3`$. Using eq.
([35](#A2.E35 "In Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle|(\overline{A}_{t}(i,j)-A(i,j)|`$ | $`\displaystyle=|\langle\overline{\omega}_{t}-\omega^{\star},\phi(i,j)\rangle|\leq\|\overline{\omega}_{t}-\omega^{\star}\|_{\Sigma_{t}}\|\phi(i,j)\|_{\Sigma_{t}^{-1}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\ \leq\left(\sigma\sqrt{d\log\left(\frac{3(1+T/\lambda)}{\delta}\right)}+\sqrt{\lambda d}\right)\|\phi(i,j)\|_{\Sigma_{t}^{-1}}=\eta_{T}\|\phi(i,j)\|_{\Sigma_{t}^{-1}}=b_{t}(i,j).`$ |  | (36) |

Here, eq.
([36](#A2.E36 "In Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from the result in eq.
([35](#A2.E35 "In Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$. Lastly
$`A^{+}_{t}(i,j)=\overline{A}_{t}(i,j)+b_{t}(i,j)`$ implies
$`0\leq A^{+}_{t}(i,j)-A(i,j)\leq 2b_{t}(i,j)`$. Similar arguments can
be used to prove eq.
([34c](#A2.E34.3 "In 34 ‣ Proposition B.1 (Optimism/Concentration). ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
 

Now Theorem
[2.1](#S2.Thmtheorem1 "Theorem 2.1. ‣ Regret Guarantees. ‣ 2.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
holds as long as for any fixed $`\delta\in[0,1]`$, for some events
$`\mathcal{E}_{\text{dep}}^{\text{matrix}}`$,
$`\mathcal{E}_{\text{ind}}^{\text{matrix}}`$ and
$`\mathcal{E}^{\text{matrix}}:=\mathcal{E}_{\text{dep}}^{\text{matrix}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\text{ind}}^{\text{matrix}}`$
with $`\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{\text{matrix}})\geq 1-\delta`$ the
following theorems can be established.

###### Theorem B.1 (Regularization-dependent guarantee).

Under
Assumptions [1](#Thmassumption1 "Assumption 1 (Linear function approximation ( , )). ‣ 2.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and
[2](#Thmassumption2 "Assumption 2 (Realizability). ‣ 2.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
for any $`\beta>0`$, reference policies
$`(\mu_{\text{ref}},\nu_{\text{ref}})`$, choosing $`\lambda=1`$ and
$`b_{t}(i,j)`$ as per eq.
([9](#S2.E9 "In Bonus Function. ‣ 2.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
in Algorithm
[1](#alg1 "Algorithm 1 ‣ Goal: Regret Minimization. ‣ 2.1 Problem Setup ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{\text{dep}}^{\text{matrix}}`$ we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall\;T\in\mathbb{N}^{+}:\qquad\mathrm{Regret}(T)\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\beta^{-1}d^{2}\left(1+\sigma^{2}\log\left(\frac{T}{\delta}\right)\right)\log\left(\frac{T}{d}\right)\right).`$ |  |

###### Theorem B.2 (Regularization-independent guarantee).

Under
Assumptions [1](#Thmassumption1 "Assumption 1 (Linear function approximation ( , )). ‣ 2.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and
[2](#Thmassumption2 "Assumption 2 (Realizability). ‣ 2.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
$`\beta\geq 0`$, reference policies
$`(\mu_{\text{ref}},\nu_{\text{ref}})`$, choosing $`\lambda=1`$ and
$`b_{t}(i,j)`$ as per eq.
([9](#S2.E9 "In Bonus Function. ‣ 2.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
in Algorithm
[1](#alg1 "Algorithm 1 ‣ Goal: Regret Minimization. ‣ 2.1 Problem Setup ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{\text{ind}}^{\text{matrix}}`$ we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall\;T\in\mathbb{N}^{+}:\qquad\mathrm{Regret}(T)\leq\mathcal{O}\left((1+\sigma)d\sqrt{T}\log\left(\frac{T}{\delta}\right)\right).`$ |  |

### B.1 Proof of Theorem [B.1](#A2.Thmtheorem1 "Theorem B.1 (Regularization-dependent guarantee). ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"): Regularization-dependent Bound

The regret can be upper bounded as follows

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | Regret(T) | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f^{{\star},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\mu_{t},{\star}}(A)\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\underbrace{\sum_{t=1}^{T}(f^{{\star},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A))}_{T_{1}}+\underbrace{\sum_{t=1}^{T}(f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(A))}_{T_{2}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{t=1}^{T}(f^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\mu_{t},\tilde{\nu}_{t}}(A))}_{T_{3}}+\underbrace{\sum_{t=1}^{T}(f^{\mu_{t},\tilde{\nu}_{t}}(A)-f^{\mu_{t},{\star}}(A))}_{T_{4}}.`$ |  | (37) |

Here we will bound the terms $`T_{1}`$ and $`T_{2}`$, the terms
$`T_{3}`$ and $`T_{4}`$ can be bounded similarly. We use
$`\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}):=\arg\max_{\mu}f^{\mu,\nu^{\prime}}(A^{\prime})`$
to denote the max player’s best-response strategy to $`\nu^{\prime}`$
under the payoff matrix $`A^{\prime}`$. Similarly, one can define
$`\nu(A^{\prime},\mu^{\prime})`$. One can derive the closed-form
expressions for the best response to $`\nu_{t}`$ under models $`A`$,
$`A^{+}_{t}`$ and $`\overline{A}_{t}`$ to be
$`\mu^{\dagger}_{t},\tilde{\mu}_{t}`$ and $`\mu_{t}`$ respectively by
solving eq.
([4](#S2.E4 "In Goal: Regret Minimization. ‣ 2.1 Problem Setup ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
to be

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mu^{\dagger}_{t,i}=\mu(A,\nu_{t})_{i}=\arg\max_{\mu}f^{\mu,\nu_{t}}(A)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mu_{\text{ref},i}\exp\left(\frac{A(i,:)\nu_{t}}{\beta}\right)\Big/Z(A,\nu_{t}),`$ |  | (38a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\tilde{\mu}_{t,i}=\mu(A^{+}_{t},\nu_{t})_{i}=\arg\max_{\mu}f^{\mu,\nu_{t}}(A^{+}_{t})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mu_{\text{ref},i}\exp\left(\frac{A^{+}_{t}(i,:)\nu_{t}}{\beta}\right)\Big/Z(A^{+}_{t},\nu_{t}),`$ |  | (38b) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mu_{t,i}=\mu(\overline{A}_{t},\nu_{t})_{i}=\arg\max_{\mu}f^{\mu,\nu_{t}}(\overline{A}_{t})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mu_{\text{ref},i}\exp\left(\frac{\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)\nu_{t}}{\beta}\right)\Big/Z(\overline{A}_{t},\nu_{t}),`$ |  | (38c) |

where

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
Z(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})=\sum_{i}\mu_{\text{ref},i}\exp\left(\frac{A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}{\beta}\right).
``` |  |

Step 1: Bounding $`T_{1}`$. From definition of the objective function
([1](#S2.E1 "In 2.1 Problem Setup ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle f^{{\star},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu^{\dagger}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}[A(i,j)]-\beta\text{KL}(\mu^{\dagger}_{t}\|\mu_{\text{ref}})-\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}[A(i,j)]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{t}\|\mu_{\text{ref}})\right)`$ |  | (39) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\beta\log(Z(A,\nu_{t}))-\beta\log(Z(A^{+}_{t},\nu_{t}))+\tilde{\mu}_{t}^{\top}(A^{+}_{t}-A)\nu_{t}`$ |  | (40) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\Delta(A^{+}_{t},\nu_{t})-\Delta(A,\nu_{t}),`$ |  |  |

where we define
$`\Delta(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})=-\beta\log(Z(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}))+\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})^{\top}(A^{\prime}-A)\nu^{\prime}`$.
Eq.
([40](#A2.E40 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from the closed-form expressions for the best responses
([38](#A2.E38 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
Using the mean-value theorem for some $`\Gamma\in[0,1]`$ with
$`A_{\Gamma}=\Gamma A^{+}_{t}+(1-\Gamma)A`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle f^{{\star},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\Delta(A^{+}_{t},\nu_{t})-\Delta(A,\nu_{t})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\frac{\partial\Delta(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}{\partial\left(A_{\Gamma}(i,:)\nu_{t}\right)}(A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\left(\beta^{-1}\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})_{i}\left[(A_{\Gamma}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i^{\prime}\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}[(A_{\Gamma}(i^{\prime},:)-A(i^{\prime},:))\nu_{t}]\right]\right)(A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}`$ |  | (41) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\left(\Gamma\beta^{-1}\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})_{i}\left[(A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i^{\prime}\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}[(A^{+}_{t}(i^{\prime},:)-A(i^{\prime},:))\nu_{t}]\right]\right)(A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\Gamma\beta^{-1}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}\left[\left((A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}\right)^{2}\right]-\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}\left[\left(A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:)\right)\nu_{t}\right]\right)^{2}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}\left[\left((A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}\right)^{2}\right].`$ |  | (42) |

Here eq.
([41](#A2.E41 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from Lemma
[B.1](#A2.Thmlemma1 "Lemma B.1. ‣ B.3 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
Letting
$`d_{t}(i)=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j\sim\nu_{t}}\left[\left(A^{+}_{t}(i,j)-A(i,j)\right)\right]`$,
we now consider the term

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle G_{1}(\Gamma)`$ | $`\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}\left[\left((A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}\right)^{2}\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j\sim\nu_{t}}\left[\left(A^{+}_{t}(i,j)-A(i,j)\right)\right]\right)^{2}\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})_{i}=\sum_{i}d_{t}(i)^{2}\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})_{i}.`$ |  | (43) |

Under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$ (Proposition
[B.1](#A2.Thmproposition1 "Proposition B.1 (Optimism/Concentration). ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\frac{\partial G_{1}(\Gamma)}{\partial\Gamma}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\left(d_{t}(i)\right)^{2}\frac{\partial\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})_{i}}{\partial\Gamma}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\left(d_{t}(i)\right)^{2}\Bigg\{\frac{\mu_{\text{ref},i}\exp\left(\beta^{-1}\left(A(i,:)\nu_{t}+\Gamma d_{t}(i)\right)\right)}{\sum_{i^{\prime}}\mu_{\text{ref},i^{\prime}}\exp\left(\beta^{-1}\left(A(i^{\prime},:)\nu_{t}\right)+\Gamma d_{t}(i^{\prime})\right)}\beta^{-1}d_{t}(i)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\frac{\mu_{\text{ref},i}\exp\left(\beta^{-1}\left(A(i,:)\nu_{t}+\Gamma d_{t}(i)\right)\right)\sum_{i^{\prime}}\beta^{-1}d_{t}(i^{\prime})\mu_{\text{ref},i^{\prime}}\exp\left(\beta^{-1}\left(A(i^{\prime},:)\nu_{t}+\Gamma d_{t}(i^{\prime})\right)\right)}{\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}}\mu_{\text{ref},i^{\prime}}\exp\left(\beta^{-1}\left(A(i^{\prime},:)\nu_{t}+\Gamma d_{t}(i^{\prime})\right)\right)\right)^{2}}\Bigg\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\beta^{-1}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}[d_{t}(i)^{3}]-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}[d_{t}(i)^{2}]\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}[d_{t}(i)]\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\beta^{-1}\text{Cov}(d_{t}(i),d_{t}(i)^{2})\geq 0.`$ |  | (44) |

Here eq.
([44](#A2.E44 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows since under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$ we have
$`d_{t}(i)\geq 0\;\forall i`$ and for any positive random variable $`X`$

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\text{Cov}(X,X^{2})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[X^{3}]-\mathbb{E}[X^{2}]\mathbb{E}[X]=\mathbb{E}\left[(X^{2})^{3/2}\right]-\mathbb{E}[X^{2}]\mathbb{E}[X]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\left(\mathbb{E}\left[X^{2}\right]\right)^{3/2}-\mathbb{E}[X^{2}]\mathbb{E}[X]=\mathbb{E}[X^{2}]\left(\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[X^{2}\right]}-\mathbb{E}[X]\right)\geq 0.`$ |  | (45) |

Thus we have $`G_{1}(\Gamma)\leq G_{1}(1)`$ and using eq.
([42](#A2.E42 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle f^{{\star},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}G_{1}(\Gamma)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}G_{1}(1)=\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A^{+}_{t},\nu_{t})}\left[\left((A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}\right)^{2}\right]`$ |  | (46) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 4\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A^{+}_{t},\nu_{t})}\left[\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j\sim\nu_{t}}[b_{t}(i,j)]\right)^{2}\right],`$ |  | (47) |

where the last inequality follows from Proposition
[B.1](#A2.Thmproposition1 "Proposition B.1 (Optimism/Concentration). ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$.

Step 2: Bounding $`T_{2}`$. From the definition of the objective
function
([1](#S2.E1 "In 2.1 Problem Setup ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}[A(i,j)]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{t}\|\mu_{\text{ref}})-\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}[A(i,j)]-\beta\text{KL}(\mu_{t}\|\mu_{\text{ref}})\right)`$ |  | (48) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\left(\beta\log(Z(A^{+}_{t},\nu_{t}))-\tilde{\mu}_{t}^{\top}(A^{+}_{t}-A)\nu_{t}\right)-\left(\beta\log(Z(\overline{A}_{t},\nu_{t}))-\mu_{t}^{\top}(\overline{A}_{t}-A)\nu_{t}\right)`$ |  | (49) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\Delta(\overline{A}_{t},\nu_{t})-\Delta(A^{+}_{t},\nu_{t}).`$ |  |  |

Eq.
([49](#A2.E49 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from the closed-form expressions for the best responses
([38](#A2.E38 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
Using the mean-value theorem for some $`\Gamma\in[0,1]`$ with
$`A_{\Gamma}=\Gamma\overline{A}_{t}+(1-\Gamma)A^{+}_{t}`$ we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\Delta(\overline{A}_{t},\nu_{t})-\Delta(A^{+}_{t},\nu_{t})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\frac{\partial\Delta(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}{\partial\left(A_{\Gamma}(i,:)\nu_{t}\right)}(\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)-A^{+}_{t}(i,:))\nu_{t}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\left(\beta^{-1}\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})_{i}\left[(A_{\Gamma}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i^{\prime}\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}[(A_{\Gamma}(i^{\prime},:)-A(i^{\prime},:))\nu_{t}]\right]\right)(\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)-A^{+}_{t}(i,:))\nu_{t}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\beta^{-1}(\mathbb{E}[XY]-\mathbb{E}[X]\mathbb{E}[Y]),`$ |  | (50) |

where the penultimate equality follows from Lemma
[B.1](#A2.Thmlemma1 "Lemma B.1. ‣ B.3 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
and in the last line we define $`X=(A_{\Gamma}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}`$,
$`Y=(\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)-A^{+}_{t}(i,:))\nu_{t}`$, and the expectation
is taken w.r.t. $`i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})`$. Note that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle X=\Gamma\underbrace{(\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}}_{\coloneqq p}+(1-\Gamma)\underbrace{(A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}}_{\coloneqq q}=\Gamma(p-q)+q,`$ |  |

and

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle Y`$ | $`\displaystyle=(\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}-(A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t}=p-q.`$ |  |

Thus

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[XY]-\mathbb{E}[X]E[Y]`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\Gamma(p-q)^{2}+q(p-q)]-\Gamma(\mathbb{E}[p-q])^{2}-\mathbb{E}[q]\mathbb{E}[(p-q)]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\Gamma\text{var}(p-q)+\text{Cov}(q,p-q)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[(p-q)^{2}]+\max\{\mathbb{E}[q^{2}],\mathbb{E}[(p-q)^{2}]\}.`$ |  | (51) |

By equations
([50](#A2.E50 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and
([51](#A2.E51 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we know that, under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}[((\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)-A^{+}_{t}(i,:))\nu_{t})^{2}]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\beta^{-1}\max\Bigg\{\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}[((\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)-A^{+}_{t}(i,:))\nu_{t})^{2}],\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}[((A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t})^{2}]\Bigg\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 5\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}\left[\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j\sim\nu_{t}}[b_{t}(i,j)]\right)^{2}\right]=5\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}\left[\left(|A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)|\nu_{t}\right)^{2}\right],`$ |  | (52) |

where the last inequality follows from the fact that
$`(|A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)|\nu_{t})=\mathbb{E}_{j\sim\nu_{t}}[b_{t}(i,j)]`$
and
$`(|A(i,:)-A^{+}_{t}(i,:)|\nu_{t})\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{j\sim\nu_{t}}[b_{t}(i,j)]=2(|A^{+}_{t}(i,:)-\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)|\nu_{t})`$
given by Proposition
[B.1](#A2.Thmproposition1 "Proposition B.1 (Optimism/Concentration). ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
One can also bound the same quantity slightly tighter as follows

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[XY]-\mathbb{E}[X]\mathbb{E}[Y]`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[p(p-q)-(1-\Gamma)(q-p)^{2}]-\mathbb{E}[p-q]\mathbb{E}[(1-\Gamma)(q-p)]-\mathbb{E}[p-q]\mathbb{E}[p]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\text{Cov}(p,p-q)-(1-\Gamma)\text{Var}(p-q)\leq\max\left\{\mathbb{E}[p^{2}],\mathbb{E}[(p-q)^{2}]\right\}`$ |  | (53) |

under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$ (c.f. Proposition
[B.1](#A2.Thmproposition1 "Proposition B.1 (Optimism/Concentration). ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
using eqs.
([50](#A2.E50 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and
([53](#A2.E53 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}\max\Bigg\{\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}[((\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)-A^{+}_{t}(i,:))\nu_{t})^{2}],\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}[((\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu_{t})^{2}]\Bigg\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}\left[\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j\sim\nu_{t}}[b_{t}(i,j)]\right)^{2}\right]=\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}\left[\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j\sim\nu_{t}}\left[A^{+}_{t}(i,j)-\overline{A}_{t}(i,j)\right]\right)^{2}\right],`$ |  | (54) |

where the last inequality follows from Proposition
[B.1](#A2.Thmproposition1 "Proposition B.1 (Optimism/Concentration). ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
Now let
$`\overline{d_{t}}(i):=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j\sim\nu_{t}}\left[A^{+}_{t}(i,j)-\overline{A}_{t}(i,j)\right]`$
and consider the term

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle G_{2}(\Gamma)`$ | $`\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}\left[\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j\sim\nu_{t}}\left[A^{+}_{t}(i,j)-\overline{A}_{t}(i,j)\right]\right)^{2}\right]=\sum_{i}\left(\overline{d_{t}}(i)\right)^{2}\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})_{i}.`$ |  | (55) |

We have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\frac{\partial G_{2}(\Gamma)}{\partial\Gamma}=\sum_{i}\left(\overline{d_{t}}(i)\right)^{2}\frac{\partial\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})_{i}}{\partial\Gamma}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\left(\overline{d_{t}}(i)\right)^{2}\Bigg\{-\frac{\mu_{\text{ref},i}\exp\left(\beta^{-1}\left(\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)\nu_{t}+(1-\Gamma)\overline{d_{t}}(i)\right)\right)}{\sum_{i^{\prime}}\mu_{\text{ref},i^{\prime}}\exp\left(\beta^{-1}\left(\overline{A}_{t}(i^{\prime},:)\nu_{t}+(1-\Gamma)\overline{d_{t}}(i^{\prime})\right)\right)}\beta^{-1}\overline{d_{t}}(i)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\frac{\mu_{\text{ref},i}\exp\left(\beta^{-1}\left(\overline{A}_{t}(i,:)\nu_{t}+(1-\Gamma)\overline{d_{t}}(i)\right)\right)\sum_{i^{\prime}}\beta^{-1}\overline{d_{t}}(i^{\prime})\mu_{\text{ref},i^{\prime}}\exp\left(\beta^{-1}\left(\overline{A}_{t}(i^{\prime},:)\nu_{t}+(1-\Gamma)\overline{d_{t}}(i^{\prime})\right)\right)}{\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}}\mu_{\text{ref},i^{\prime}}\exp\left(\beta^{-1}\left(\overline{A}_{t}(i^{\prime},:)\nu_{t}+(1-\Gamma)\overline{d_{t}}(i^{\prime})\right)\right)\right)^{2}}\Bigg\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=-\beta^{-1}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}\left[\left(\overline{d_{t}}(i)\right)^{3}\right]-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}\left[\left(\overline{d_{t}}(i)\right)^{2}\right]\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}\Big[\overline{d_{t}}(i)\Big]\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=-\beta^{-1}\text{Cov}(\overline{d_{t}}(i)^{2},\overline{d_{t}}(i))\leq 0,`$ |  | (56) |

last line follows since under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$ we have
$`\overline{d_{t}}(i)\geq 0\;\forall i`$ and for any positive random
variable $`X`$ using eq.
([45](#A2.E45 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have $`\text{Cov}(X,X^{2})\geq 0`$ Thus the term
$`G_{2}(\Gamma)\leq G_{2}(0)`$. Hence from eq.
([54](#A2.E54 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle T_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle=f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A_{\Gamma},\nu_{t})}\left[\left(\overline{d_{t}}(i)]\right)^{2}\right]=\beta^{-1}G_{2}(\Gamma)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}G_{2}(0)=\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A^{+}_{t},\nu_{t})}\left[\left(\overline{d_{t}}(i)\right)^{2}\right]=\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A^{+}_{t},\nu_{t})}\left[\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j\sim\nu_{t}}[b_{t}(i,j)]\right)^{2}\right].`$ |  | (57) |

Step 3: Finishing up. From equations
([47](#A2.E47 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and
([57](#A2.E57 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
w.p. $`1-\delta/3`$ (Under event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$) we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle T_{1}+T_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq 5\beta^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu(A^{+}_{t},\nu_{t})}\left[\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j\sim\nu_{t}}[b_{t}(i,j)]\right)^{2}\right]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 5\beta^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}\left[\left(b_{t}(i,j)\right)^{2}\right].`$ |  |

Similarly w.p. $`1-\delta/3`$ (Under event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$), using
the same arguments as above for the min player, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle T_{3}+T_{4}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq 5\beta^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{t}\\
j\sim\tilde{\nu}_{t}\end{subarray}}\left[\left(b_{t}(i,j)\right)^{2}\right].`$ |  |

Define

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\Sigma_{t}^{+}:=\lambda\mathbf{I}+\sum_{(i,j)\in{\mathcal{D}^{+}_{t-1}}}\phi(i,j)\phi(i,j)^{\top}\quad\text{and}\quad\Sigma_{t}^{-}=\lambda\mathbf{I}+\sum_{(i,j)\in{\mathcal{D}^{-}_{t-1}}}\phi(i,j)\phi(i,j)^{\top}.`$ |  | (58) |

By defining the filtration
$`\mathcal{F}_{t-1}=\sigma\left(\left\{(i_{l}^{+},j_{l}^{+},\widehat{A}(i_{l}^{+},j_{l}^{+})),(i_{l}^{-},j_{l}^{-},\widehat{A}(i_{l}^{-},j_{l}^{-}))\right\}_{l=1}^{t-1}\right)`$,
we observe that random variables
$`\left\|\phi\left(i_{t}^{+},j_{t}^{+}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}^{2}`$
and
$`\left\|\phi\left(i_{t}^{-},j_{t}^{-}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{t}^{-}\right)^{-1}}^{2}`$
are $`\mathcal{F}_{t}`$-measurable, while the policies
$`\tilde{\mu}_{t},\mu_{t},\tilde{\nu}_{t}`$ and $`\nu_{t}`$ are
$`\mathcal{F}_{t-1}`$ measurable. Define the events

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}\|\phi(i,j)\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{+})^{-1}}^{2}\leq 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\phi(i_{t}^{+},j_{t}^{+})\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{+})^{-1}}^{2}+8\log\left(\frac{12}{\delta}\right)\right\},`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{3}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{t}\\
j\sim\tilde{\nu}_{t}\end{subarray}}\|\phi(i,j)\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{-})^{-1}}^{2}\leq 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\phi(i_{t}^{-},j_{t}^{-})\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{-})^{-1}}^{2}+8\log\left(\frac{12}{\delta}\right)\right\}.`$ |  |

Choosing $`\lambda=1`$ and using Lemma
[A.2](#A1.Thmlemma2 "Lemma A.2 (Martingale Concentration, Lemma B.2 in [ ] ). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
with $`R=1`$ (since
$`\|\phi(i,j)\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{-})^{-1}}^{2}\leq 1\;\forall\;(i,j)\in[m]\times[n]`$
from
Assumption [1](#Thmassumption1 "Assumption 1 (Linear function approximation ( , )). ‣ 2.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
we have $`\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{2})\geq 1-\delta/6`$ and
$`\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{3})\geq 1-\delta/6`$. Thus from
([37](#A2.E37 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
under the event
$`\mathcal{E}_{\text{dep}}^{\text{matrix}}:=\mathcal{E}_{1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2}\cap\mathcal{E}_{3}`$,
we have the dual gap bounded as

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | Regret(T) | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f^{{\star},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\mu_{t},{\star}}(A)\right)=5\beta^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}\left[\left(b_{t}(i,j)\right)^{2}\right]+5\beta^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{t}\\
j\sim\tilde{\nu}_{t}\end{subarray}}\left[\left(b_{t}(i,j)\right)^{2}\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=5\beta^{-1}\eta^{2}_{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}\|\phi(i,j)\|_{\Sigma_{t}^{-1}}^{2}+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{t}\\
j\sim\tilde{\nu}_{t}\end{subarray}}\|\phi(i,j)\|_{\Sigma_{t}^{-1}}^{2}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 5\beta^{-1}\eta^{2}_{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}\|\phi(i,j)\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{+})^{-1}}^{2}+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{t}\\
j\sim\tilde{\nu}_{t}\end{subarray}}\|\phi(i,j)\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{-})^{-1}}^{2}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 10\beta^{-1}\eta^{2}_{T}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\|\phi(i_{t}^{+},j_{t}^{+})\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{+})^{-1}}^{2}+\|\phi(i_{t}^{-},j_{t}^{-})\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{-})^{-1}}^{2}\right)+8\log(12\delta^{-1})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\beta^{-1}\left(1+\sigma\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{T}{\delta}\right)}+\sigma^{2}\log\left(\frac{T}{\delta}\right)\right)d^{2}\log\left(\frac{T}{d}\right)\right),`$ |  | (59) |

where the third line follows from the fact
$`\Sigma_{t}^{+}\preceq\Sigma_{t}`$ and
$`\Sigma_{t}^{-}\preceq\Sigma_{t}`$, the penultimate line comes from
event $`\mathcal{E}_{3}\cap\mathcal{E}_{3}`$. Setting $`\lambda=1`$, we
use the elliptical potential lemma (Lemma
[A.6](#A1.Thmlemma6 "Lemma A.6 (Elliptical Potential Lemma). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
to obtain the last line.

### B.2 Proof of Theorem [B.2](#A2.Thmtheorem2 "Theorem B.2 (Regularization-independent guarantee). ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"): Regularization-independent Bound

Using eq.
([37](#A2.E37 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have $`\text{Regret}(T)=T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}+T_{4}`$ and $`T_{3}+T_{4}`$
can be bound similar to $`T_{1}+T_{2}`$. Let $`\mu^{\dagger}_{t}`$ be
the best response to $`\nu_{t}`$ under $`A`$ (c.f.
([38](#A2.E38 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))).
We bound $`T_{1}`$ using UCB style analysis, under the event
$`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$, as follows:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle T_{1}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}(f^{\mu^{\dagger}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A))\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}(f^{\mu^{\dagger}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A^{+}_{t})-f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A))`$ |  | (60) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}(f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A^{+}_{t})-f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A))=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}[A^{+}_{t}(i,j)-A(i,j)]`$ |  | (61) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}[b_{t}(i,j)]=2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}[\left\|\phi(i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{t}^{-1}}]\leq 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}\left\|\phi(i,j)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}.`$ |  | (62) |

Eq.
([60](#A2.E60 "In B.2 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-independent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and the first inequality in
([62](#A2.E62 "In B.2 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-independent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follow from the Proposition
[B.1](#A2.Thmproposition1 "Proposition B.1 (Optimism/Concentration). ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
Here
([61](#A2.E61 "In B.2 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-independent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows since
$`\tilde{\mu}_{t}=\arg\max\limits_{\mu}f^{\mu,\nu_{t}}(A^{+}_{t})`$. The
second inequality in eq.
([62](#A2.E62 "In B.2 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-independent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
comes from the fact $`\Sigma_{t}^{+}\preceq\Sigma_{t}`$. Similarly,
under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}`$, we can bound $`T_{2}`$ as follows

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle T_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}(f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(A))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}(f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A)-f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(\overline{A}_{t}))+\sum_{t=1}^{T}(f^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(\overline{A}_{t})-f^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(A))`$ |  | (63) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}[b_{t}(i,j)]+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}[b_{t}(i,j)]`$ |  | (64) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}[b_{t}(i,j)]=2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\eta_{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}[\left\|\phi(i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{t}^{-1}}]`$ |  | (65) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2\eta_{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}\left\|\phi(i,j)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{t}^{+}\right)^{-1}},`$ |  | (66) |

where
([63](#A2.E63 "In B.2 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-independent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from the fact that
$`\mu_{t}=\arg\max\limits_{\mu}f^{\mu,\nu_{t}}(\overline{A}_{t})`$,
([64](#A2.E64 "In B.2 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-independent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from Proposition
[B.1](#A2.Thmproposition1 "Proposition B.1 (Optimism/Concentration). ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
([65](#A2.E65 "In B.2 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-independent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows since
$`f^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(\overline{A}_{t})\geq f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(\overline{A}_{t})`$
and

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
f^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(\overline{A}_{t})+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}[b_{t}(i,j)]=f^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(A^{+}_{t})\leq f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(A^{+}_{t})=f^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(\overline{A}_{t})+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}[b_{t}(i,j)],
``` |  |

and
([66](#A2.E66 "In B.2 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-independent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from the fact $`\Sigma_{t}^{+}\preceq\Sigma_{t}`$. Define the
filtration

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathcal{F}_{t-1}=\sigma\left(\left\{(i_{l}^{+},j_{l}^{+},\widehat{A}(i_{l}^{+},j_{l}^{+})),(i_{l}^{-},j_{l}^{-},\widehat{A}(i_{l}^{-},j_{l}^{-}))\right\}_{l=1}^{t-1}\right).
``` |  |

We have random variable
$`\left\|\phi\left(i_{t}^{+},j_{t}^{+}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}`$
is $`\mathcal{F}_{t}`$-measurable, while the policies
$`\tilde{\mu}_{t},\mu_{t},\tilde{\nu}_{t}`$ and $`\nu_{t}`$ are
$`\mathcal{F}_{t-1}`$ measurable. Define the events

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{4}=\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}\left[\|\phi(i,j)\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{+})^{-1}}\right]\leq 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\phi(i_{t}^{+},j_{t}^{+})\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{+})^{-1}}+8\log\left(\frac{12}{\delta}\right)\right\},`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{5}=\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{t}\\
j\sim\tilde{\nu}_{t}\end{subarray}}\left[\|\phi(i,j)\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{-})^{-1}}\right]\leq 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\phi(i_{t}^{-},j_{t}^{-})\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{-})^{-1}}+8\log\left(\frac{12}{\delta}\right)\right\}.`$ |  |

Choosing $`\lambda=1`$ we have
$`\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{4})\geq 1-\delta/6`$ and
$`\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{5})\geq 1-\delta/6`$ using Lemma
[A.2](#A1.Thmlemma2 "Lemma A.2 (Martingale Concentration, Lemma B.2 in [ ] ). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
with $`R=1`$ (since $`\|\phi(i,j)\|_{(\Sigma_{t}^{-})^{-1}}\leq 1`$
$`\forall\;(i,j)\in[m]\times[n]`$ from assumption
[1](#Thmassumption1 "Assumption 1 (Linear function approximation ( , )). ‣ 2.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
Under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{4}`$, using equations
([62](#A2.E62 "In B.2 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-independent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and
([66](#A2.E66 "In B.2 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-independent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle T_{1}+T_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq 4\eta_{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{t}\\
j\sim\nu_{t}\end{subarray}}\left[\left\|\phi(i,j)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 8\eta_{T}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\phi(i_{t}^{+},j_{t}^{+})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}+4\log\left(\frac{12}{\delta}\right)\right)`$ |  | (67) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 8\eta_{T}\left(\sqrt{T\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\phi(i_{t}^{+},j_{t}^{+})\right\|^{2}_{\left(\Sigma_{t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}}+4\log\left(\frac{12}{\delta}\right)\right)=\mathcal{O}\left((1+\sigma)d\sqrt{T}\log\left(\frac{T}{\delta}\right)\right).`$ |  | (68) |

The equations
([67](#A2.E67 "In B.2 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-independent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and
([68](#A2.E68 "In B.2 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-independent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follow from event $`\mathcal{E}_{4}`$ and Lemma
[A.6](#A1.Thmlemma6 "Lemma A.6 (Elliptical Potential Lemma). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
(elliptical potential lemma) respectively. Similarly one can bound
$`T_{3}+T_{4}`$ under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{5}`$
by
$`\mathcal{O}\left(\sigma d\sqrt{T}\log\left(\frac{T}{\delta}\right)\right)`$.
Thus under the event
$`\mathcal{E}_{\text{ind}}^{\text{matrix}}:=\mathcal{E}_{1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{4}\cap\mathcal{E}_{5}`$,
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\text{Regret}(T)\leq\mathcal{O}\left((1+\sigma)d\sqrt{T}\log\left(\frac{T}{\delta}\right)\right).`$ |  | (69) |

Finally under the event
$`\mathcal{E}^{\text{matrix}}=\mathcal{E}_{\text{dep}}^{\text{matrix}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\text{ind}}^{\text{matrix}}=\mathcal{E}_{1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2}\cap\mathcal{E}_{3}\cap\mathcal{E}_{4}\cap\mathcal{E}_{5}`$
(w.p. atleast $`1-\delta`$) equations eqs.
([59](#A2.E59 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and
([69](#A2.E69 "In B.2 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-independent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
hold simultaneously which completes the proof of Theorem
[2.1](#S2.Thmtheorem1 "Theorem 2.1. ‣ Regret Guarantees. ‣ 2.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").

### B.3 Auxiliary Lemmas

###### Lemma B.1.

The partial derivative
$`\frac{\partial\Delta(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})}{\partial A^{\prime}(i:)\nu^{\prime}}`$
is given by

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\frac{\partial\Delta(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})}{\partial A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\beta^{-1}\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})_{i}(A^{\prime}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu^{\prime}-\beta^{-1}\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})_{i}\sum_{i^{\prime}}\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})_{i^{\prime}}(A^{\prime}(i^{\prime},:)-A(i^{\prime},:))\nu^{\prime}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\beta^{-1}\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})_{i}\left[(A^{\prime}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu^{\prime}-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i^{\prime}\sim\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})}[(A^{\prime}(i^{\prime},:)-A(i^{\prime},:))\nu^{\prime}]\right].`$ |  | (70) |

Proof. The symbol
$`\frac{\partial}{\partial\!A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}`$ denotes
differentiation with respect to the *scalar* quantity
$`A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}`$. Throughout this differentiation we
regard the vector $`\nu^{\prime}`$ as constant, and keep every row of
$`A^{\prime}`$ *except* the $`i^{\text{th}}`$ row fixed. Because the
other rows are held fixed, the cross-derivatives vanish:
$`\frac{\partial\!A^{\prime}(i^{\prime},:)\nu^{\prime}}{\partial\!A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}=0,\;\forall i^{\prime}\neq i,`$
so each row contributes an independent gradient term.

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\frac{\partial\Delta(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})}{\partial A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{\partial\left[-\beta\log(Z(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}))+\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})(A^{\prime}-A)\nu^{\prime}\right]}{\partial A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=-\frac{\beta}{Z(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})}\frac{\partial Z(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})}{\partial A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}+[\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})]_{i}+\frac{\partial\left([\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})]_{i}\right)}{\partial A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}(A^{\prime}(i,:)-A(i,:))\nu^{\prime}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i^{\prime}\neq i}\frac{\partial[\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})]_{i^{\prime}}}{\partial A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}(A^{\prime}(i^{\prime},:)-A(i^{\prime},:))\nu^{\prime}.`$ |  | (71) |

We have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\frac{\partial Z(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})}{\partial\bigl(A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}\bigr)}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mu_{\text{ref},i}\,\exp\!\Bigl(\tfrac{A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}{\beta}\Bigr)\,\frac{1}{\beta}=\frac{Z(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})}{\beta}[\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})]_{i},`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\frac{\partial\left([\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})]_{i}\right)}{\partial A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{\partial\left(\mu_{\text{ref},i}\,\exp\left(A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}/\beta\right)/Z(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})\right)}{\partial A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\frac{\beta^{-1}\left(\mu_{\text{ref},i}\,\exp\left(A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}/\beta\right)Z(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})-(\mu_{\text{ref},i}\,\exp\left(A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}/\beta\right)^{2}\right)}{Z(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})^{2}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\beta^{-1}\left([\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})]_{i}-[\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})]_{i}^{2}\right),`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\frac{\partial\left([\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})]_{i^{\prime}}\right)}{\partial A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{\partial\left(\mu_{\text{ref},i^{\prime}}\,\exp\left(A^{\prime}(i^{\prime},:)\nu^{\prime}/\beta\right)/Z(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})\right)}{\partial A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\frac{-\beta^{-1}\left(\mu_{\text{ref},i}\,\exp\left(A^{\prime}(i,:)\nu^{\prime}/\beta\right)\mu_{\text{ref},i^{\prime}}\,\exp\left(A^{\prime}(i^{\prime},:)\nu^{\prime}/\beta\right)\right)}{Z(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})^{2}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=-\beta^{-1}[\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})]_{i}[\mu(A^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})]_{i^{\prime}}.`$ |  |

Substituting back in eq.
([71](#A2.E71 "In B.3 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we get the desired result.  

## Appendix C Markov Game Proofs

##### Notation and Convention.

For any function $`f:\mathcal{S}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}`$ we define
$`P_{h}f(s,i,j):=\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim P_{h}(\cdot|s,i,j)}[f(s^{\prime})]`$.
We also use the notation

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{s_{h+1}|s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}}(f(s_{h+1})):=\mathbb{E}_{s_{h+1}\sim P_{h}(\cdot|s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})}[f(s_{h+1})]=P_{h}f(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}).
``` |  |

For all $`K>H`$ and
$`(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}`$, we set
$`\widehat{Q}_{K}(s,i,j)=0`$, $`\widehat{V}_{K}(s)=0`$,
$`\text{KL}(\widehat{\mu}_{H+1}(\cdot|s)\|\mu_{\text{ref},K}(\cdot|s))=0`$,
and
$`\text{KL}(\widehat{\nu}_{K}(\cdot|s)\|\nu_{\text{ref},K}(\cdot|s))=0`$.
These conventions apply to every value function $`\widehat{V}`$, every
$`Q`$-function $`\widehat{Q}`$ (both estimates and true values), and all
feasible policies $`\widehat{\mu}`$ and $`\widehat{\nu}`$.

###### Proposition C.1.

The closed-form expressions of the best response to min-player strategy
$`\nu^{\prime}`$ under for a $`Q`$ function $`Q_{h}^{\prime}(s,i,j)`$
$`\forall(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}},h\in[H]`$
denoted by $`\mu(Q^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})`$ where
$`Q^{\prime}:=\left\{Q_{h}^{\prime}\right\}_{h=1}^{H}`$ is given by

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle[\mu_{h,t}(Q^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})](i|s)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{\mu_{\text{ref},h}(i|s)\exp\left(\mathbb{E}_{j\sim\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s)}[Q^{\prime}(s,i,j)/\beta]\right)}{\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}}\mu_{\text{ref},h}(i^{\prime}|s)\exp\left(\mathbb{E}_{j\sim\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s)}[Q^{\prime}(s,i^{\prime},j)/\beta]\right)}`$ |  |

and we have $`\mu_{t}=\mu(\overline{Q}_{t},\nu_{t})`$,
$`\tilde{\mu}_{t}=\mu(Q^{+}_{t},\nu_{t})`$ and
$`\mu^{\dagger}_{t}=\mu(Q^{\mu^{\dagger}_{t},\nu_{t}},\nu_{t})`$

Proof. The result is an immediate consequence of the definitions and
routine calculations.  

Now in order to prove our main result we note that Theorem
[3.1](#S3.Thmtheorem1 "Theorem 3.1. ‣ Regret Guarantees. ‣ 3.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
holds as long as for any $`\delta\in[0,1]`$, Theorems
[C.1](#A3.Thmtheorem1 "Theorem C.1 (Logarithmic regret guarantee). ‣ Notation and Convention. ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and
[C.2](#A3.Thmtheorem2 "Theorem C.2 (Traditional √𝑇 guarantee). ‣ Notation and Convention. ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
can be established.

###### Theorem C.1 (Logarithmic regret guarantee).

Under Assumption
[3](#Thmassumption3 "Assumption 3 (Linear MDP ( ) ). ‣ 3.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
for any fixed $`\delta\in[0,1]`$ and any $`\beta>0`$, reference policies
$`(\mu_{\text{ref}},\nu_{\text{ref}})=(\left\{\mu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|\cdot)\right\}_{h=1}^{H},\left\{\nu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|\cdot)\right\}_{h=1}^{H})`$,
choosing $`\lambda=1`$ and $`b^{\texttt{sup}}_{h,t}(s,i,j)`$ as per eq.
([24](#S3.E24 "In Bonus Function. ‣ 3.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
in Algorithm
[2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall\;T\in\mathbb{N}^{+}:\qquad\mathrm{Regret}(T)\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\beta^{-1}d^{3}H^{7}\log\left(\frac{dT}{\delta}\right)\log\left(\frac{dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)\right)\quad\mathrm{w.p.}\;1-\delta/2.`$ |  |

###### Theorem C.2 (Traditional $`\sqrt{T}`$ guarantee).

Under Assumption
[3](#Thmassumption3 "Assumption 3 (Linear MDP ( ) ). ‣ 3.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
for any fixed $`\delta\in[0,1]`$ and any $`\beta>0`$, reference policies
$`(\mu_{\text{ref}},\nu_{\text{ref}})=(\left\{\mu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|\cdot)\right\}_{h=1}^{H},\left\{\nu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|\cdot)\right\}_{h=1}^{H})`$,
choosing $`\lambda=1`$ and $`b^{\texttt{sup}}_{h,t}(s,i,j)`$ as per eq.
([24](#S3.E24 "In Bonus Function. ‣ 3.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
in Algorithm
[2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\forall\;T\in\mathbb{N}^{+}:\qquad\mathrm{Regret}(T)\leq\mathcal{O}\left(d^{3/2}H^{3}\sqrt{T}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{dT}{\delta}\right)\log\left(\frac{dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)\cdot}\right)\quad\mathrm{w.p.}\;1-\delta/2.`$ |  |

### C.1 Supporting Lemmas

We begin by introducing some lemmas that will be used in proving the
main result. The proofs of these lemmas are deferred to Section
[C.4](#A3.SS4 "C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").

In Lemmas
[C.1](#A3.Thmlemma1 "Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
[C.2](#A3.Thmlemma2 "Lemma C.2 (Concentration of Superoptimistic Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and Corollary
[C.1](#A3.Thmcorollary1 "Corollary C.1 (Bounds on Superoptimistic Bellman error w.r.t. the 𝑄^+ function). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
we introduce high probability concentration events and Bellman error
bounds used in proving our main results.

###### Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors).

Define the Bellman error of the MSE $`Q`$ function as

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{e}_{h,t}(s,i,j):=\overline{Q}_{h,t}(s,i,j)-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1}(s,i,j).`$ |  | (72) |

Then under the setting in Algorithm
[2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
choosing $`\lambda=1`$,
$`\forall(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}},h\in[H]`$,
$`\forall\;t\in[T]`$ the event

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{6}\coloneqq\left\{|\overline{e}_{h,t}(s,i,j)|\leq{\eta_{1}}\|\phi(s,i,j)\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}\coloneqq b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h,t}(s,i,j)\right\}`$ |  | (73) |

occurs with probability at least $`1-\delta/16`$. Here
$`\eta_{1}\coloneqq c_{1}\sqrt{d}H\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16HT}{\delta}\right)}`$,
where $`c_{1}>0`$ is a universal constant.

###### Lemma C.2 (Concentration of Superoptimistic Bellman errors).

Under the setting in Algorithm
[2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
choosing $`\lambda=1`$,
$`\forall(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}},h\in[H]`$,
$`\forall\;t\in[T]`$ the event

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{7}:=\left\{\left|\left\langle\theta_{h,t}^{+},\phi(s,i,j)\right\rangle-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}V^{+}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\right|\leq{\eta_{2}}\|\phi(s,i,j)\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}\coloneqq b_{h,t}(s,i,j)\right\}`$ |  |

occurs with probability $`1-\delta/16`$. Here
$`\eta_{2}=c_{2}dH^{2}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)}`$
and $`c_{2}>0`$ is a universal constant.

###### Corollary C.1 (Bounds on Superoptimistic Bellman error w.r.t. the $`Q^{+}`$ function).

Let

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
e^{+}_{h,t}(s,i,j):=Q_{h,t}^{+}(s,i,j)-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}V^{+}_{h+1}(s,i,j),
``` |  |

then under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{7}`$, for
$`b^{\texttt{sup}}_{h,t}(s,i,j):=b_{h,t}(s,i,j)+2b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h,t}(s,i,j)`$,
we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|e^{+}_{h,t}(s,i,j)\right|\leq 2b_{h,t}(s,i,j)+2b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h,t}(s,i,j)=b^{\texttt{sup}}_{h,t}(s,i,j)+b_{h,t}(s,i,j).`$ |  |

For notational simplicity, while stating the next two lemmas we will
omit the superscript $`\nu_{t}`$ and also the dependence on $`t`$. Both
lemmas are valid for all $`t\in[T]`$. Consequently, the symbols $`\mu`$
and $`\tilde{\mu}`$ in
Lemma [C.3](#A3.Thmlemma3 "Lemma C.3 (Optimism). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and
Lemma [C.4](#A3.Thmlemma4 "Lemma C.4 (Super-optimistic gap). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
should be interpreted as the time-indexed policies $`\mu_{t}`$ and
$`\tilde{\mu}_{t}`$, rather than an arbitrary policies.

Lemma
[C.3](#A3.Thmlemma3 "Lemma C.3 (Optimism). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
formalizes the notion of optimism for
Algorithm [2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").

###### Lemma C.3 (Optimism).

For the setting in Algorithm
[2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{6}\cap\mathcal{E}_{7}`$,
$`\forall(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\in\mathcal{S\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}},h\in[H+1]`$
and any policy $`\mu^{\prime}`$ of the max player, we have the following
equations hold:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle Q^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq\overline{Q}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}),`$ |  | (74a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle Q^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}).`$ |  | (74b) |

The next lemma introduces the concept of the superoptimistic gap,
arising from the construction of the superoptimistic bonus term and the
projection operators.

###### Lemma C.4 (Super-optimistic gap).

For the setting in Algorithm
[2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{6}\cap\mathcal{E}_{7}`$,
$`\forall(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\in\mathcal{S\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}},h\in[H+1]`$,
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle 2\left|\left(Q^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-\overline{Q}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\right)\right|\geq\left|Q^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-Q_{h}^{\mu}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\right|.`$ |  | (75) |

Note that this is the exact condition used in the matrix games section
that we use to bound the term $`T_{2}`$ using an expectation of some
function over actions sampled using the best-response policy
$`\tilde{\mu}`$ using the first bounding method
([51](#A2.E51 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).

### C.2 Proof of Theorem [C.1](#A3.Thmtheorem1 "Theorem C.1 (Logarithmic regret guarantee). ‣ Notation and Convention. ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"): Logarithmic Regret Bound

For simplicity we fix the initial state to $`s_{1}`$, extending the
arguments to a fixed initial distribution $`s_{1}\sim\rho`$ is trivial.
One step regret is given by

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\textsf{DualGap}(\mu_{t},\nu_{t})`$ | $`\displaystyle=V_{1}^{{\star},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})-V_{1}^{\mu_{t},{\star}}(s_{1})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\underbrace{V_{1}^{{\star},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})-V_{1}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})}_{T_{5}^{(t)}}+\underbrace{V_{1}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})-V_{1}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})}_{T_{6}^{(t)}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\underbrace{V_{1}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})-V_{1}^{\mu_{t},\tilde{\nu}_{t}}(s_{1})}_{T_{7}^{(t)}}+\underbrace{V_{1}^{\mu_{t},\tilde{\nu}_{t}}(s_{1})-V_{1}^{\mu_{t},{\star}}(s_{1})}_{T_{8}^{(t)}}.`$ |  | (76) |

Below we bound $`T_{5}^{(t)}`$ and $`T_{6}^{(t)}`$, and the remaining
two terms can be bounded similarly.

##### Step 1: Bounding $`T_{5}^{(t)}`$.

For notational simplicity we will omit the superscript $`\nu_{t}`$ here
as we try to bound both $`T_{5}`$ and $`T_{6}`$. Given a fixed strategy
of the minimizing player one can treat the best response computation
objective as a RL policy optimization. Let $`\mu^{\dagger}_{t}`$ denote
the best response to $`\tilde{\nu}_{t}`$ at $`t`$. We will use the
following leafing here inspired from [Zhao et al. \[2025b\]](#bib.bib1).
Let $`\mu^{(h)}:=\tilde{\mu}_{1:h}\oplus\mu^{\dagger}_{h+1:H}`$ denote
the concatenated policy that plays $`\tilde{\mu}`$ for the first $`h`$
steps and then executes $`\mu^{\dagger}`$ for the remaining steps. Again
we drop the subscript $`t`$ here for notational simplicity. Consider the
term

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle T_{5}`$ | $`\displaystyle=V_{1}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s_{1})-V_{1}^{\tilde{\mu}}(s_{1})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{h=0}^{H-1}\underbrace{V_{1}^{\mu^{(h)}}(s_{1})-V_{1}^{\mu^{(h+1)}}(s_{1})}_{I_{h+1}}.`$ |  |

For any policy pair $`(\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})`$, $`h\in[H]`$, let
$`d_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}`$ denote the state distribution
induced at step $`h`$ when following the policy
$`(\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})`$. Under the event
$`\mathcal{E}_{6}\cap\mathcal{E}_{7}`$, we can bound each $`I_{h+1}`$ as
follows

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle I_{h+1}=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\left[V_{h+1}^{\mu^{(h)}}(s_{h+1})-V_{h+1}^{\mu^{(h+1)}}(s_{h+1})\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\mu^{\dagger}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{h+1}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\beta\text{KL}(\mu^{\dagger}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1}))\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\quad-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{h+1}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1}))\right]`$ |  | (77) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i_{h+1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})}\left[\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})}\left[Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-Q_{h+1}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]\right)^{2}\right]`$ |  | (78) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[\left(Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-Q_{h+1}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right)^{2}\right].`$ |  |  |

Note that here
([77](#A3.E77 "In Step 1: Bounding 𝑇_5^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from the fact
$`Q_{h+1}^{\mu^{(h)}}(s,i,j)=Q_{h+1}^{\mu^{(h+1)}}(s,i,j)=r_{h+1}(s,i,j)+P_{h+1}V_{h+2}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s,i,j)=Q_{h+1}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s,i,j)`$
$`\forall(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}},h\in[H]`$.
Eq.
([78](#A3.E78 "In Step 1: Bounding 𝑇_5^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
comes from (for
$`Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\geq Q_{h+1}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})`$)
Lemma
[C.3](#A3.Thmlemma3 "Lemma C.3 (Optimism). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and the same analysis used for bounding the term $`T_{1}`$ (see eqs.
([39](#A2.E39 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))-([46](#A2.E46 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))).
Here $`Q_{h+1}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s_{h+1},\cdot,\cdot)`$ will be mapped to
$`A(\cdot,\cdot)`$ and $`Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},\cdot,\cdot)`$ to
$`A^{+}(\cdot,\cdot)`$ from the matrix games section. Let
$`a_{h+1}=(i_{h+1},j_{h+1})`$, now using Lemma
[C.3](#A3.Thmlemma3 "Lemma C.3 (Optimism). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle 0`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-Q_{h+1}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+2}|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}\left(V_{h+2}^{+}(s_{h+2})-V_{h+2}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s_{h+2})\right)+e_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+2}|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+2}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\\
j_{h+2}\sim\nu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\end{subarray}}\big(Q_{h+2}^{+}(s_{h+2},i_{h+2},j_{h+2})-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2}))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\beta\text{KL}(\nu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\|\nu_{\text{ref},h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2}))\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+2}|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+2}\sim\mu^{\dagger}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\\
j_{h+2}\sim\nu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\end{subarray}}\big(Q_{h+2}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s_{h+2},i_{h+2},j_{h+2})-\beta\text{KL}(\mu^{\dagger}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2}))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\beta\text{KL}(\nu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\|\nu_{\text{ref},h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2}))\big)+e_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+2}|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+2}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\\
j_{h+2}\sim\nu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{h+2}^{+}(s_{h+2},i_{h+2},j_{h+2})-Q_{h+2}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s_{h+2},i_{h+2},j_{h+2})\right]+e_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})`$ |  | (79) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\cdots`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{.|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}\left[\sum_{k=h+1}^{H}e_{k}^{+}(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k})\right].`$ |  |  |

Here $`\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}_{.|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}`$ denotes
expectation with respect to the law of
$`s_{k}\sim\tilde{\mu},\nu|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}`$, that is, the distribution
of $`s_{k}`$ induced by policy $`(\tilde{\mu},\nu)`$ when starting from
state $`s_{h+1}`$, taking action $`a_{h+1}`$ at step $`h+1`$ ,
$`i_{k}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{k}(\cdot|s_{k})`$ and
$`j_{k}\sim\nu_{k}(\cdot|s_{k})`$ for $`k>h+1`$. Here
$`e^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ is the Bellman error of the optimistic
Q function and the Bellman error of
$`Q^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})=r_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+P_{h}V^{\mu^{\dagger}}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$
is zero. Eq.
([79](#A3.E79 "In Step 1: Bounding 𝑇_5^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows by lower bounding the second term by swapping
$`\mu^{\dagger}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})`$ to the policy
$`\tilde{\mu}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})`$ since

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mu^{\dagger}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})=\arg\max_{\mu^{\prime}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+2}\sim\mu^{\prime}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\\
j_{h+2}\sim\nu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\end{subarray}}\left(Q_{h+2}^{\mu^{\dagger}}(s_{h+2},i_{h+2},j_{h+2})-\beta\text{KL}(\mu^{\prime}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+2})\right).
``` |  |

Thus we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle I_{h+1}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{.|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}\sum_{k=h+1}^{H}e_{k}^{+}(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k})\right)^{2}\right]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}\left[\left(\sum_{k=h+1}^{H}e_{k}^{+}(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k})\right)^{2}\right].`$ |  |

Here $`\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}`$ is used to denote
$`s_{k}\sim d_{k}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}`$,
$`i_{k}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{k}(\cdot|s_{k})`$ and
$`j_{k}\sim\nu_{k}(\cdot|s_{k})`$. Thus, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle T_{5}=\sum_{h=0}^{H-1}I_{h+1}\leq\beta^{-1}\sum_{h=0}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}\left[\left(\sum_{k=h+1}^{H}e_{k}^{+}(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k})\right)^{2}\right].`$ |  | (80) |

##### Step 2: Bounding $`T_{6}^{(t)}`$.

Similar to bounding $`T_{5}`$ we leaf the policy in the following. Let
$`\mu^{(h)}=\tilde{\mu}_{1:h}\oplus\mu_{h+1:H}`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle T_{6}`$ | $`\displaystyle=V_{1}^{\tilde{\mu}}(s_{1})-V_{1}^{\mu}(s_{1})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{h=0}^{H-1}\underbrace{V_{1}^{\mu^{(H-h)}}(s_{1})-V_{1}^{\mu^{(H-h-1)}}(s_{1})}_{J_{H-h-1}}.`$ |  | (81) |

We can write $`J_{h}`$ ($`h=0,\cdots,H-1`$) as follows

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle J_{h}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\left[V_{h+1}^{\mu^{(h+1)}}(s_{h+1})-V_{h+1}^{\mu^{(h)}}(s_{h+1})\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{h+1}^{\mu}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1}))\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\mu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{h+1}^{\mu}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\beta\text{KL}(\mu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1}))\right].`$ |  | (82) |

Note that here eq.
([82](#A3.E82 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from the fact
$`Q_{h+1}^{\mu^{(h)}}(s,i,j)=Q_{h+1}^{\mu^{(h+1)}}(s,i,j)=r_{h+1}(s,i,j)+P_{h+1}V_{h+2}^{\mu}(s,i,j)=Q_{h+1}^{\mu}(s,i,j)`$
$`\forall(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}},h\in[H]`$.
Now under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{6}\cap\mathcal{E}_{7}`$,
$`\exists\;\Gamma\in[0,1]`$ such that, for

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
g_{1}(s_{h+1}):=\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i_{h+1}\sim\mu_{h+1}^{\Gamma}(\cdot|s_{h+1})}\left[\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})}\left[Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]\right)^{2}\right],
``` |  |

and

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
g_{2}(s_{h+1}):=\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i_{h+1}\sim\mu_{h+1}^{\Gamma}(\cdot|s_{h+1})}\left[\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})}\left[Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-Q^{\mu}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]\right)^{2}\right].
``` |  |

we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle J_{h}\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\left[g_{1}(s_{h+1})+\max\{g_{1}(s_{h+1}),g_{2}(s_{h+1})\}\right].`$ |  | (83) |

Here eq..
([83](#A3.E83 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
is obtained using the same arguments as the matrix games section,
specifically the first way of bounding $`T_{2}`$ (see
eqs.([48](#A2.E48 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))-([51](#A2.E51 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))).
Here we can map eq.
([82](#A3.E82 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
to the eq.
([48](#A2.E48 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
specifically $`Q_{h+1}^{\mu}(s_{h+1},\cdot,\cdot)`$ can be mapped to
$`A(\cdot,\cdot)`$, $`Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},\cdot,\cdot)`$ to
$`A^{+}(\cdot,\cdot)`$ and $`\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},\cdot,\cdot)`$
to $`\overline{A}(\cdot,\cdot)`$ from the matrix games section. The
policy $`\mu_{h+1}^{\Gamma}(\cdot|s_{h+1})`$ is the optimal best
response to $`\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})`$ under the reward model
$`Q_{h+1}^{\Gamma}(\cdot|s_{h+1})`$
($`\mu_{h+1}^{\Gamma}:=\mu(Q^{\Gamma},\nu)`$, see Proposition
[C.1](#A3.Thmproposition1 "Proposition C.1. ‣ Notation and Convention. ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
where

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle Q_{h+1}^{\Gamma}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\Gamma\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})+(1-\Gamma)Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})+(1-\Gamma)\left(Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right)`$ |  |

Now using Lemma
[C.4](#A3.Thmlemma4 "Lemma C.4 (Super-optimistic gap). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle g_{2}(s_{h+1})\leq 4\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i_{h+1}\sim\mu_{h+1}^{\Gamma}(\cdot|s_{h+1})}\left[\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})}\left[Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]\right)^{2}\right].`$ |  |

and thus

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
````
