````math
|  | $`\displaystyle J_{h}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq 5\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i_{h+1}\sim\mu_{h+1}^{\Gamma}(\cdot|s_{h+1})}\left[\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})}\left[Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]\right)^{2}\right]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 5\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\mu_{h+1}^{\Gamma}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[\left(Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right)^{2}\right].`$ |  |

Note that this is the exact form we obtain while bounding the term
$`T_{2}`$ and using the same arguments
([55](#A2.E55 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))-([57](#A2.E57 "In B.1 Proof of Theorem : Regularization-dependent Bound ‣ Appendix B Matrix Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
one can show that the term is maximized at $`\Gamma=0`$ and we have
$`\mu_{h+1}^{0}=\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}`$, specifically
$`Q_{h+1}^{\mu}(s_{h+1},\cdot,\cdot)`$ will be mapped to
$`A(\cdot,\cdot)`$, $`Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},\cdot,\cdot)`$ to
$`A^{+}(\cdot,\cdot)`$, $`Q_{h+1}^{\Gamma}(s_{h+1},\cdot,\cdot)`$ will
be mapped to $`A_{\Gamma}(\cdot,\cdot)`$ and
$`\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},\cdot,\cdot)`$ to
$`\overline{A}(\cdot,\cdot)`$.

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle J_{h}\leq 5\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[\left(Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right)^{2}\right].`$ |  | (84) |

Let $`a_{h+1}=(i_{h+1},j_{h+1})`$, using Lemma
[C.3](#A3.Thmlemma3 "Lemma C.3 (Optimism). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle 0`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq Q_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})`$ |  | (85) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+2}|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}\left(V_{h+2}^{+}(s_{h+2})-\overline{V}_{h+2}(s_{h+2})\right)+e_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\overline{e}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+2}|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+2}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\\
j_{h+2}\sim\nu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\end{subarray}}\Big(Q_{h+2}^{+}(s_{h+2},i_{h+2},j_{h+2})-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2}))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\beta\text{KL}(\nu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\|\nu_{\text{ref},h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2}))\Big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+2}|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+2}\sim\mu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\\
j_{h+2}\sim\nu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\end{subarray}}\big(\overline{Q}_{h+2}(s_{h+2},i_{h+2},j_{h+2})-\beta\text{KL}(\mu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2}))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\beta\text{KL}(\nu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\|\nu_{\text{ref},h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2}))\big)+e_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\overline{e}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+2}|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+2}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\\
j_{h+2}\sim\nu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{h+2}^{+}(s_{h+2},i_{h+2},j_{h+2})-\overline{Q}_{h+2}(s_{h+2},i_{h+2},j_{h+2})\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+e_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\overline{e}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})`$ |  | (86) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\cdots`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{.|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}\left[\sum_{k=h+1}^{H}e_{k}^{+}(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k})-\overline{e}_{k}(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k})\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}_{.|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}\left[\sum_{k=h+1}^{H}\left|e_{k}^{+}(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k})\right|\right]+\mathbb{E}_{.|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}\left[\sum_{k=h+1}^{H}\left|\overline{e}_{k}(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k})\right|\right]\right).`$ |  | (87) |

Here eq.
([86](#A3.E86 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from lower bounding the second term by swapping the policy
$`\mu`$ by $`\tilde{\mu}`$ since $`\mu`$ is the maximizer under
$`\overline{Q}(\cdot|s_{h+2})`$

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})=\arg\max_{\mu^{\prime}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+2}\sim\mu^{\prime}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\\
j_{h+2}\sim\nu_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\end{subarray}}\left(\overline{Q}_{h+2}(s_{h+2},i_{h+2},j_{h+2})-\beta\text{KL}(\mu^{\prime}_{h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+2}(\cdot|s_{h+2}))\right)
``` |  |

Thus combining equations
([84](#A3.E84 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and
([87](#A3.E87 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle J_{h}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq 5\beta^{-1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}\sim d_{h+1}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{.|s_{h+1},a_{h+1}}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}\left[\sum_{k=h+1}^{H}\left|e_{k}^{+}(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k})\right|+\sum_{k=h+1}^{H}\left|\overline{e}_{k}(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k})\right|\right]\right)^{2}\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 5\beta^{-1}\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}\left[\left(\sum_{k=h}^{H}\left|e_{k}^{+}\left(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k}\right)\right|+\left|\overline{e}_{k}\left(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k}\right)\right|\right)^{2}\right].`$ |  | (88) |

Here $`\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu},\nu}`$ is used to denote
$`s_{k}\sim d_{k}^{\mu,\nu}`$, $`i_{k}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{k}(\cdot|s_{k})`$
and $`j_{k}\sim\nu_{k}(\cdot|s_{k})`$.

##### Step 3: Finishing up.

Define

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}:=\lambda\mathbf{I}+\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}^{+}}\phi(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})\phi(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})^{\top}\quad\text{and}\quad\Sigma_{h,t}^{-}:=\lambda\mathbf{I}+\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}^{-}}\phi(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})\phi(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})^{\top}.`$ |  |

By defining the filtration
$`\mathcal{F}_{t-1}=\sigma\left(\{\tau_{l}^{+},\tau_{l}^{-}\}_{l=1}^{t-1}\right)`$,
where
$`\tau^{+}_{t}=\left\{(s_{h,t}^{+},i_{h,t}^{+},j_{h,t}^{+},r_{h,t}^{+},s_{h+1,t}^{+})\right\}_{h=1}^{H}`$
and
$`\tau^{-}_{t}=\left\{(s_{h,t}^{-},i_{h,t}^{-},j_{h,t}^{-},r_{h,t}^{-},s_{h+1,t}^{-})\right\}_{h=1}^{H}`$
as defined in Algorithm
[2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
we observe that the random variable
$`\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\phi\left(s_{h,t}^{+},i_{h,t}^{+},j_{h,t}^{+}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}^{2}`$
is $`\mathcal{F}_{t}`$ measurable while the policies $`\tilde{\mu}_{t}`$
and $`\nu_{t}`$ are $`\mathcal{F}_{t-1}`$ measurable. Now let
$`\mathcal{E}_{8}`$ denote the event

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{8}=\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\phi\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}^{2}\right]\leq 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\phi\left(s_{h,t}^{+},i_{h,t}^{+},j_{h,t}^{+}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}^{2}+8H\log\left(\frac{16}{\delta}\right)\right\}.`$ |  |

Then choosing $`\lambda=1`$,
$`\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{8})\geq 1-\delta/8`$ using Lemma
[A.2](#A1.Thmlemma2 "Lemma A.2 (Martingale Concentration, Lemma B.2 in [ ] ). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
with $`R=H`$ since
$`\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\phi\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}^{2}\leq H`$
by assumption
[3](#Thmassumption3 "Assumption 3 (Linear MDP ( ) ). ‣ 3.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
Now under the event
$`\mathcal{E}_{6}\cap\mathcal{E}_{7}\cap\mathcal{E}_{8}`$ (w.p. at least
$`1-\delta/4`$), combining equations
([80](#A3.E80 "In Step 1: Bounding 𝑇_5^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
([81](#A3.E81 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),([88](#A3.E88 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and bringing back the $`t`$ in the superscript we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}(T_{5}^{(t)}+T_{6}^{(t)})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(5\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\left(\sum_{k=h}^{H}\left|e_{k,t}^{+}\left(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k}\right)\right|+\left|\overline{e}_{k,t}\left(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k}\right)\right|\right)^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\left(\sum_{k=h}^{H}\left|e^{+}_{k,t}\left(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k}\right)\right|\right)^{2}\right]\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(5\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\left(\sum_{k=h}^{H}2b_{k,t}\left(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k}\right)+3b^{\texttt{mse}}_{k,t}\left(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k}\right)\right)^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\left(\sum_{k=h}^{H}b_{k,t}\left(s_{k},i_{k},j_{k}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\right)`$ |  | (89) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\beta^{-1}H^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(5\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\left(2b_{h,t}\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)+3b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h,t}\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)\right)^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\left(b_{h,t}\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq c_{3}\beta^{-1}d^{2}H^{6}\log\left(\frac{16dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\left\|\phi\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}^{2}\right]`$ |  | (90) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq c_{3}\beta^{-1}d^{2}H^{6}\log\left(\frac{16dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\left\|\phi\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}^{2}\right]`$ |  | (91) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2c_{3}\beta^{-1}d^{2}H^{6}\log\left(\frac{16dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\phi\left(s_{h,t}^{+},i_{h,t}^{+},j_{h,t}^{+}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}^{2}\right)+4H\log\left(\frac{16}{\delta}\right)\right)`$ |  | (92) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq c_{3}^{\prime}\beta^{-1}d^{3}H^{7}\log\left(\frac{16dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)\log\left(\frac{T+1}{\delta}\right).`$ |  | (93) |

Here we use Corollary
[C.1](#A3.Thmcorollary1 "Corollary C.1 (Bounds on Superoptimistic Bellman error w.r.t. the 𝑄^+ function). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and Lemma
[C.1](#A3.Thmlemma1 "Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
to obtain eq.
([89](#A3.E89 "In Step 3: Finishing up. ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
Eq.
([90](#A3.E90 "In Step 3: Finishing up. ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
can be derived for some universal constant $`c_{3}`$ by substituting the
expressions for $`b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h,t}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ and
$`b_{h,t}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$. Eq.
([91](#A3.E91 "In Step 3: Finishing up. ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
relies on the identity
$`\Sigma_{h,t}=\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}+\Sigma_{h,t}^{-}`$, which implies that
$`\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\preceq\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}`$. Eq.
([92](#A3.E92 "In Step 3: Finishing up. ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
from event $`\mathcal{E}_{8}`$. Eq.
([93](#A3.E93 "In Step 3: Finishing up. ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from the elliptical potential lemma (Lemma
[A.6](#A1.Thmlemma6 "Lemma A.6 (Elliptical Potential Lemma). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
One can similarly bound the term
$`\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(T_{7}^{(t)}+T_{8}^{(t)}\right)`$ (w.p.
$`1-\delta/4`$) to obtain

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\text{Regret}(T)=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\textsf{DualGap}(\mu_{t},\nu_{t})\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\beta^{-1}d^{3}H^{7}\log\left(\frac{dT}{\delta}\right)\log\left(\frac{dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)\right)\quad\mathrm{w.p.}\;(1-\delta/2).`$ |  |

### C.3 Proof of Theorem [C.2](#A3.Thmtheorem2 "Theorem C.2 (Traditional √𝑇 guarantee). ‣ Notation and Convention. ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"): Traditional $`\sqrt{T}`$ bound

#### C.3.1 $`\beta>0`$: Regularized setting

For simplicity we again fix the initial state to $`s_{1}`$, extending
the arguments to a fixed initial distribution $`s_{1}\sim\rho`$ is
trivial. Recall the dual gap can be decomposed as
$`\textsf{DualGap}(\mu_{t},\nu_{t})=T_{5}^{(t)}+T_{6}^{(t)}+T_{7}^{(t)}+T_{8}^{(t)}`$
as per equation
([76](#A3.E76 "In C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
We will bound the terms $`T_{5}^{(t)}`$ and $`T_{6}^{(t)}`$ and the
remaining terms can be bounded similarly.

##### Step 1: Bounding $`T_{5}^{(t)}`$.

Let $`\mu^{\dagger}_{t}`$ denote the best response to $`\nu_{t}`$ at
time $`t`$. We shall omit $`\nu_{t}`$ in the superscript of $`Q`$ for
notational simplicity. Then under the event
$`\mathcal{E}_{6}\cap\mathcal{E}_{7}`$ we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle T_{5}^{(t)}`$ | $`\displaystyle=V_{1}^{{\star},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})-V_{1}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\mu^{\dagger}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{1}^{\mu^{\dagger}_{t}}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\mu^{\dagger}_{1,t}(\cdot\|s_{1})||\mu_{\text{ref},1}(\cdot\|s_{1}))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{1}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t}}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot\|s_{1})||\mu_{\text{ref},1}(\cdot\|s_{1}))\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\mu^{\dagger}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{1,t}^{+}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\mu^{\dagger}_{1,t}(\cdot\|s_{1})||\mu_{\text{ref},1}(\cdot\|s_{1}))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{1}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t}}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot\|s_{1})||\mu_{\text{ref},1}(\cdot\|s_{1}))\right)`$ |  | (94) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{1,t}^{+}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{1}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t}}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]`$ |  | (95) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[P_{1}V^{+}_{2,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[P_{1}V^{\tilde{\mu}_{t}}_{2,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[e^{+}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[V_{2,t}^{+}(s_{2})-V_{2,t}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t}}(s_{2})\right]+\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[e^{+}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\cdots`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}e_{h,t}^{+}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\right].`$ |  | (96) |

Here eq.
([94](#A3.E94 "In Step 1: Bounding 𝑇_5^(𝑡). ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from optimism (Lemma
[C.3](#A3.Thmlemma3 "Lemma C.3 (Optimism). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and eq.
([95](#A3.E95 "In Step 1: Bounding 𝑇_5^(𝑡). ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows since $`\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})`$ is the optimal policy
under $`Q_{1}^{+}(s_{1},\cdot,\cdot)`$.

##### Step 2: Bounding $`T_{6}^{(t)}`$.

We have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle T_{6}^{(t)}`$ | $`\displaystyle=V_{1}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})-V_{1}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\underbrace{V_{1}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})-\overline{V}_{1,t}(s_{1})}_{T_{6a}^{(t)}}+\underbrace{\overline{V}_{1,t}(s_{1})-V_{1}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})}_{T_{6b}^{(t)}}.`$ |  |

Here we again omit $`\nu_{t}`$ in the superscript for notational
simplicity. Under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{6}\cap\mathcal{E}_{7}`$, the
term $`T_{6a}^{(t)}`$ can be bounded as follows

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle T_{6a}^{(t)}`$ | $`\displaystyle=V_{1}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})-\overline{V}_{1,t}(s_{1})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{\tilde{\mu}_{t}}_{1}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot\|s_{1})||\mu_{\text{ref},1}(\cdot\|s_{1}))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\mu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[\overline{Q}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\mu_{1,t}(\cdot\|s_{1})||\mu_{\text{ref},1}(\cdot\|s_{1}))\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot\|s_{1})||\mu_{\text{ref},1}(\cdot\|s_{1}))-`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[\overline{Q}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{t}(\cdot\|s_{1})||\mu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot\|s_{1}))\right)`$ |  | (97) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})-\overline{Q}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right].`$ |  | (98) |

Here eq.
([97](#A3.E97 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows by upper bounding $`Q_{1}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t}}`$ by $`Q_{1,t}^{+}`$
using optimism (Lemma
[C.3](#A3.Thmlemma3 "Lemma C.3 (Optimism). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
in the first (positive) term and lower bounding the second (negative)
term by switching the max players policy to
$`\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})`$ since

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})=\arg\max_{\mu^{\prime}_{1}(\cdot|s_{1})}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\mu^{\prime}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{i}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[\overline{Q}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\mu^{\prime}_{1}(\cdot\|s_{1})||\mu_{\text{ref},1}(\cdot\|s_{1}))\right)
``` |  |

is the optimal policy under $`\overline{Q}_{1,t}`$. Under the event
$`\mathcal{E}_{6}\cap\mathcal{E}_{7}`$, we bound $`T_{6b}^{(t)}`$ as
follows

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle T_{6b}^{(t)}=\overline{V}_{1,t}(s_{1})-V_{1}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s_{1})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\mu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{1}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[\overline{Q}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})-Q_{1}^{\mu_{t}}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\mu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{1}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})-\overline{Q}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]`$ |  | (99) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\mu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{1}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\mu_{1,t}(\cdot\|s_{1})||\mu_{\text{ref},1}(\cdot\|s_{1}))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\mu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{1}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[\overline{Q}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\mu_{1,t}(\cdot\|s_{1})||\mu_{\text{ref},1}(\cdot\|s_{1}))\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{1}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{t}(\cdot\|s_{1})||\mu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot\|s_{1}))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{1}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[\overline{Q}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot\|s_{1})||\mu_{\text{ref},1}(\cdot\|s_{1}))\right)`$ |  | (100) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{1}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})-\overline{Q}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right].`$ |  | (101) |

Here eq.
([99](#A3.E99 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from Lemma
[C.4](#A3.Thmlemma4 "Lemma C.4 (Super-optimistic gap). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and Lemma
[C.3](#A3.Thmlemma3 "Lemma C.3 (Optimism). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
Eq.
([100](#A3.E100 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows by upper bounding the first term and lower bounding the second
term by swapping policy $`\mu_{t}(\cdot\|s_{1})`$ by
$`\tilde{\mu}_{t}(\cdot\|s_{1})`$ since
$`\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot\|s_{1})`$ is the optimal policy under
$`Q_{1,t}^{+}(s_{1},\cdot,\cdot)`$ and $`\mu_{t}(\cdot\|s_{1})`$ is the
optimal policy under $`\overline{Q}_{1,t}(s_{1},\cdot,\cdot)`$. From
equations
([98](#A3.E98 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and
([101](#A3.E101 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
under the event $`\mathcal{E}_{6}\cap\mathcal{E}_{7}`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle T_{6}^{(t)}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq 2\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\\
j_{1}\sim\nu_{1,t}(\cdot|s_{1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})-\overline{Q}_{1,t}(s_{1},i_{1},j_{1})\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{H}\left|e_{h,t}^{+}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\right|\right]+\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left|\overline{e}_{h,t}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\right|\right]\right).`$ |  | (102) |

Here eq.
([102](#A3.E102 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
can be obtained using the same steps used in obtaining equations
([85](#A3.E85 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))-([87](#A3.E87 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).

##### Step 3: Finishing up.

By defining the filtration
$`\mathcal{F}_{t-1}=\sigma\left(\{\tau_{l}^{+},\tau_{l}^{-}\}_{l=1}^{t-1}\right)`$,
we observe that the random variable
$`\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\phi\left(s_{h,t}^{+},i_{h,t}^{+},j_{h,t}^{+}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}`$
is $`\mathcal{F}_{t}`$ measurable while the policies $`\tilde{\mu}_{t}`$
and $`\nu_{t}`$ are $`\mathcal{F}_{t-1}`$ measurable. Now let
$`\mathcal{E}_{9}`$ denote the event

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{9}=\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\phi\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}\right]\leq 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\phi\left(s_{h,t}^{+},i_{h,t}^{+},j_{h,t}^{+}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}+8H\log\left(\frac{16}{\delta}\right)\right\}.`$ |  |

Then choosing $`\lambda=1`$,
$`\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{9})\geq 1-\delta/8`$ by Lemma
[A.2](#A1.Thmlemma2 "Lemma A.2 (Martingale Concentration, Lemma B.2 in [ ] ). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
with $`R=H`$ since
$`\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\phi\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}\leq H`$
by assumption
[3](#Thmassumption3 "Assumption 3 (Linear MDP ( ) ). ‣ 3.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
Now using equations
([96](#A3.E96 "In Step 1: Bounding 𝑇_5^(𝑡). ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and
([102](#A3.E102 "In Step 2: Bounding 𝑇_6^(𝑡). ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
under the event
$`\mathcal{E}_{6}\cap\mathcal{E}_{7}\cap\mathcal{E}_{9}`$ (w.p.
$`1-\delta/4`$) we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(T_{5}^{(t)}+T_{6}^{(t)}\right)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(3\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left|e_{h,t}^{+}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\right|\right]+2\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left|\overline{e}_{h,t}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\right|\right]\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(3\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(2b_{h,t}\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)+2b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h,t}\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)\right)\right]+2\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h,t}\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)\right]\right)`$ |  | (103) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq c_{4}dH^{2}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\left\|\phi\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}\right]`$ |  | (104) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq c_{4}dH^{2}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}^{\tilde{\mu}_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\left\|\phi\left(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}\right]`$ |  | (105) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2c_{4}dH^{2}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\phi\left(s_{h,t}^{+},i_{h,t}^{+},j_{h,t}^{+}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}+4H\log\left(\frac{16}{\delta}\right)\right)`$ |  | (106) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2c_{4}dH^{2}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)}\left(\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sqrt{T\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\phi\left(s_{h,t}^{+},i_{h,t}^{+},j_{h,t}^{+}\right)\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}\right)^{-1}}^{2}}+4H\log\left(\frac{16}{\delta}\right)\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq c_{4}^{\prime}dH^{3}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)}\left(\sqrt{dT\log(T+1)}+4\log\left(\frac{16}{\delta}\right)\right).`$ |  | (107) |

Here we use Corollary
[C.1](#A3.Thmcorollary1 "Corollary C.1 (Bounds on Superoptimistic Bellman error w.r.t. the 𝑄^+ function). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and Lemma
[C.1](#A3.Thmlemma1 "Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
to obtain eq.
([103](#A3.E103 "In Step 3: Finishing up. ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
Eq.
([104](#A3.E104 "In Step 3: Finishing up. ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
can be derived for some universal constant $`c_{4}`$ by substituting the
expressions for $`b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h,t}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ and
$`b_{h,t}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$. Eq.
([105](#A3.E105 "In Step 3: Finishing up. ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
uses the fact $`\Sigma_{h,t}\succeq\Sigma_{h,t}^{+}`$. The bound in
([106](#A3.E106 "In Step 3: Finishing up. ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from event $`\mathcal{E}_{9}`$. Eq.
([107](#A3.E107 "In Step 3: Finishing up. ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from the elliptical potential lemma (Lemma
[A.6](#A1.Thmlemma6 "Lemma A.6 (Elliptical Potential Lemma). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
One can similarly bound the term
$`\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(T_{7}^{(t)}+T_{8}^{(t)}\right)`$ (w.p.
$`1-\delta/4`$) to obtain

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\text{Regret}(T)=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\textsf{DualGap}(\mu_{t},\nu_{t})\leq\mathcal{O}\left(d^{3/2}H^{3}\sqrt{T}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{dT}{\delta}\right)\log\left(\frac{dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)\cdot}\right)\quad\mathrm{w.p.}\;(1-\delta/2).`$ |  |

### C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas

#### C.4.1 Proof of Lemma [C.1](#A3.Thmlemma1 "Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")

Using Lemma
[A.8](#A1.Thmlemma8 "Lemma A.8 (Lemma D.4 in [ ] ). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
with the covering number bound in Lemma
[C.10](#A3.Thmlemma10 "Lemma C.10 (Covering number of the MSE Value function class in Algorithm ). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
$`B_{1}=H`$ (from Lemma
[C.6](#A3.Thmlemma6 "Lemma C.6. ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
$`L=2H\sqrt{2dt/\lambda}`$ (from Lemma
[C.9](#A3.Thmlemma9 "Lemma C.9 (𝐿_2 norm bounds). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
$`B_{3}=0`$, we have with probability at least $`1-\delta/16H`$
$`\forall\;t\in[T]`$ ,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,t}\left[\overline{V}_{h+1,t}\left(s_{h+1}^{\tau}\right)-P_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})\right]\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}^{2}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 4H^{2}\left[\frac{d}{2}\log\left(\frac{2t+\lambda}{\lambda}\right)+d\log\left(1+\frac{8H\sqrt{2dt}}{\varepsilon\sqrt{\lambda}}\right)+\log\left(\frac{16H}{\delta}\right)\right]+\frac{32t^{2}\varepsilon^{2}}{\lambda}.`$ |  |

Choosing $`\lambda=1`$ and $`\varepsilon=\sqrt{d}H/t`$, we have
$`\forall\;h\in[H]`$ and $`\forall\;t\in[T]`$

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,t}\left[\overline{V}_{h+1,t}\left(s_{h+1}^{\tau}\right)-P_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})\right]\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{d}H\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16HT}{\delta}\right)},`$ |  | (108) |

for some universal constant $`C_{1}>0`$. Note that we have to divide the
$`\delta/16`$ budget across the horizon since at every $`h\in[H]`$ the
function class used while applying lemma
[A.8](#A1.Thmlemma8 "Lemma A.8 (Lemma D.4 in [ ] ). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
is different as a result of the projection operation varying with $`h`$.
Since $`r_{h}(s,i,j)+P_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\in[0,H-h+1]`$ from
Lemma [C.6](#A3.Thmlemma6 "Lemma C.6. ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
and
$`\overline{Q}_{h,t}(s,i,j)=\Pi_{h}(\langle\overline{\theta}_{h,t},\phi(s,i,j)\rangle)`$,
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\overline{Q}_{h,t}(s,i,j)-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\right|\leq\left|\langle\overline{\theta}_{h,t},\phi(s,i,j)\rangle-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\right|.`$ |  | (109) |

Now let $`\pi^{\star}=(\mu^{\star},\nu^{\star})`$ be the nash
equilibrium policy of the true MDP, and $`\theta_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}`$ be
its corresponding parameter, whose existence is guaranteed by Lemma
[C.8](#A3.Thmlemma8 "Lemma C.8 (Linearity of the Q function). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\theta_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\left(\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}\phi_{h,\tau}^{\top}+\lambda\mathbf{I}\right)\theta^{\pi^{\star}}_{h}=\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\left(\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}(r_{h,\tau}+P_{h}V_{h+1,t}^{\pi^{\star}})+\lambda\theta^{\pi^{\star}}_{h}\right).`$ |  | (110) |

Also recall

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{\theta}_{h,t}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}\left[r_{h,\tau}+\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s^{\tau}_{h+1})\right].`$ |  |

Using the above two equations we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{\theta}_{h,t}-\theta_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\left\{\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}\left[\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s^{\tau}_{h+1})-P_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})\right]-\lambda\theta_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\underbrace{-\lambda\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\theta_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}}_{p_{1}}+\underbrace{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}\left[\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s^{\tau}_{h+1})-P_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})\right]}_{p_{2}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\underbrace{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}\left[P_{h}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})\right)\right]}_{p_{3}}.`$ |  | (111) |

Assuming eq.
([108](#A3.E108 "In C.4.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
holds (w.p. $`1-\delta/16`$), one can bound the terms as follows:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{1}\rangle\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle=\left|\langle\phi(s,i,j),\lambda\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\theta_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}\rangle\right|\leq\lambda\left\|\theta_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}\left\|\phi(s,i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}\leq 2H\sqrt{d\lambda}\left\|\phi(s,i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}},`$ |  | (112a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{2}\rangle\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{d}H\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16HT}{\delta}\right)}\left\|\phi(s,i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}.`$ |  | (112b) |
| Here eq. ([112a](#A3.E112.1 "In 112 ‣ C.4.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")) follows from Lemma [C.8](#A3.Thmlemma8 "Lemma C.8 (Linearity of the Q function). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"). We use the result from eq. ([108](#A3.E108 "In C.4.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")) to obtain upper bound in eq. ([112b](#A3.E112.2 "In 112 ‣ C.4.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")). Lastly we have |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{3}\rangle`$ | $`\displaystyle=\left\langle\phi(s,i,j),\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}\left[P_{h}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})\right)\right]\right\rangle`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\left\langle\phi(s,i,j),\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}(\phi_{h,\tau})^{\top}\left[\int\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s^{\prime})-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}(s^{\prime})\right)d\psi(s^{\prime})\right]\right\rangle`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\left\langle\phi(s,i,j),\int\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s^{\prime})-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}(s^{\prime})\right)d\psi(s^{\prime})\right\rangle`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad-\lambda\left\langle\phi(s,i,j),\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\int\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s^{\prime})-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}(s^{\prime})\right)d\psi(s^{\prime})\right\rangle`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=P_{h}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,t}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}\right)(s,i,j)-\lambda\left\langle\phi(s,i,j),\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\int\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s^{\prime})-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}(s^{\prime})\right)d\psi(s^{\prime})\right\rangle.`$ |  |  |
| Thus |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{3}\rangle-P_{h}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,t}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}\right)(s,i,j)\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle=\left|-\lambda\left\langle\phi(s,i,j),\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\int\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s^{\prime})-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}(s^{\prime})\right)d\psi(s^{\prime})\right\rangle\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2H\sqrt{d\lambda}\left\|\phi(s,i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}`$ |  | (112c) |

Here eq.
([112c](#A3.E112.3 "In 112 ‣ C.4.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from Lemma
[C.6](#A3.Thmlemma6 "Lemma C.6. ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and Lemma
[C.5](#A3.Thmlemma5 "Lemma C.5. ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
Now

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\langle\overline{\theta}_{h,t},\phi(s,i,j)\rangle-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1}(s,i,j)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\langle\overline{\theta}_{h,t},\phi(s,i,j)\rangle-Q_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}(s,i,j)-P_{h}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,t}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}\right)(s,i,j)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\left\langle\phi(s,i,j),\overline{\theta}_{h,t}-\theta_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}\right\rangle-P_{h}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,t}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}\right)(s,i,j)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:diff_thetas}}{=}\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{1}\rangle+\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{2}\rangle+\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{3}\rangle-P_{h}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,t}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}\right)(s,i,j).`$ |  | (113) |

Using the equations
([112a](#A3.E112.1 "In 112 ‣ C.4.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),([112b](#A3.E112.2 "In 112 ‣ C.4.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
([112c](#A3.E112.3 "In 112 ‣ C.4.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
([113](#A3.E113 "In C.4.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\langle\overline{\theta}_{h,t},\phi(s,i,j)\rangle-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\right|\leq c_{1}\sqrt{d}H\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16HT}{\delta}\right)}\left\|\phi(s,i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}`$ |  |

for some universal constant $`c_{1}>0`$. Using eq.
([109](#A3.E109 "In C.4.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
completes the proof

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\overline{Q}_{h,t}(s,i,j)-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\left|\langle\overline{\theta}_{h,t},\phi(s,i,j)\rangle-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\right|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq c_{1}\sqrt{d}H\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16HT}{\delta}\right)}\left\|\phi(s,i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}.`$ |  |

#### C.4.2 Proof of Lemma [C.2](#A3.Thmlemma2 "Lemma C.2 (Concentration of Superoptimistic Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")

Using Lemma
[A.8](#A1.Thmlemma8 "Lemma A.8 (Lemma D.4 in [ ] ). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
with with the covering number bound in Lemma
[C.11](#A3.Thmlemma11 "Lemma C.11 (Covering number of the superoptimistic value function class in Algorithm ). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
$`B_{1}=4H^{2}`$ (from Lemma
[C.7](#A3.Thmlemma7 "Lemma C.7 (Range of Q, V functions). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
$`L=2H\sqrt{2dt/\lambda}`$ (from Lemma
[C.9](#A3.Thmlemma9 "Lemma C.9 (𝐿_2 norm bounds). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
$`L^{+}=4H^{2}\sqrt{2dt/\lambda}`$ (from Lemma
[C.9](#A3.Thmlemma9 "Lemma C.9 (𝐿_2 norm bounds). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
$`B_{2}^{+}+B_{2}-b_{2}\leq 4H^{2}`$ (from equation
([21](#S3.E21 "In 1st item ‣ 3.2 Algorithm Development ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")))
and $`B_{3}=\eta_{2}+2\eta_{1}`$ we have with probability at least
$`1-\delta/16H`$ $`\forall\;t\in[T]`$

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,t}\left[V^{+}_{h+1,t}\left(s_{h+1}^{\tau}\right)-P_{h}V_{h+1,t}^{+}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})\right]\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}^{2}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\leq 64H^{4}\Bigg[\frac{d}{2}\log\left(\frac{2t+\lambda}{\lambda}\right)+d\log\left(1+\frac{48H^{2}\sqrt{2dt}}{\varepsilon\sqrt{\lambda}}\right)+d^{2}\log\left(1+\frac{72\sqrt{d}(\eta_{2}+2\eta_{1})^{2}}{\lambda\varepsilon^{2}}\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+d\log\left(1+\frac{48H\sqrt{2dt}}{\varepsilon\sqrt{\lambda}}\right)+d\log\left(1+\frac{4608H^{5}\sqrt{2dt}}{\varepsilon^{2}\beta\sqrt{\lambda}}\right)+\log\left(\frac{16H}{\delta}\right)\Bigg]+\frac{32t^{2}\varepsilon^{2}}{\lambda},`$ |  |

Setting $`\lambda=1`$ and
$`\eta_{1}=c_{1}\sqrt{d}H\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16TH}{\delta}\right)}`$ ,
$`\varepsilon=dH^{2}/T`$ and
$`\eta_{2}=c_{2}dH^{2}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16dTH}{\min\{1,\beta\}\delta}\right)}`$,
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,t}\left[V^{+}_{h+1,t}\left(s_{h+1}^{\tau}\right)-P_{h}V_{h+1,t}^{+}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})\right]\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}\leq C_{2}dH^{2}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16((c_{2}+2c_{1})+1)dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)},`$ |  | (114) |

for some universal constant $`C_{2}>0`$. Using the same steps as used in
the proof of Lemma
[C.1](#A3.Thmlemma1 "Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
we have $`\forall\;h\in[H]`$ and $`t\in[T]`$

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\theta^{+}_{h,t}-\theta_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\underbrace{-\lambda\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\theta_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}}_{p_{4}}+\underbrace{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}\left[V^{+}_{h+1,t}(s^{\tau}_{h+1})-P_{h}V^{+}_{h+1,t}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})\right]}_{p_{5}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\underbrace{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}\left[P_{h}\left(V^{+}_{h+1,t}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})\right)\right]}_{p_{6}}.`$ |  |

Assuming eq.
([114](#A3.E114 "In C.4.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
holds (w.p. $`1-\delta/16`$), one can bound the terms as follows

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{4}\rangle\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle=\left|\phi(s,i,j),\lambda\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\theta_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}\right|\leq\lambda\left\|\theta_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}\left\|\phi(s,i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}\leq 2H\sqrt{d\lambda}\left\|\phi(s,i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}},`$ |  | (115a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{5}\rangle\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{2}dH^{2}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16((c_{2}+2c_{1})+1)dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)}\left\|\phi(s,i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}.`$ |  | (115b) |
| Here eq. ([115a](#A3.E115.1 "In 115 ‣ C.4.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")) follows from Lemma [C.8](#A3.Thmlemma8 "Lemma C.8 (Linearity of the Q function). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"). We use the result from eq. ([114](#A3.E114 "In C.4.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")) to obtain upper bound in eq. ([115b](#A3.E115.2 "In 115 ‣ C.4.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")) Lastly using similar arguments as Lemma ([C.1](#A3.Thmlemma1 "Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")) we have |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{6}\rangle`$ | $`\displaystyle=\left\langle\phi(s,i,j),\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}\left[P_{h}\left(V^{+}_{h+1,t}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}(s_{h}^{\tau},i_{h}^{\tau},j_{h}^{\tau})\right)\right]\right\rangle`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=P_{h}\left(V^{+}_{h+1,t}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}\right)(s,i,j)-\quad\lambda\left\langle\phi(s,i,j),\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\int\left(V^{+}_{h+1,t}(s^{\prime})-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}(s^{\prime})\right)d\psi(s^{\prime})\right\rangle.`$ |  |  |
| Thus |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{6}\rangle-P_{h}\left(V^{+}_{h+1,t}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}\right)(s,i,j)\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle=\left|-\lambda\left\langle\phi(s,i,j),\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\int\left(V^{+}_{h+1,t}(s^{\prime})-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{\star}}(s^{\prime})\right)d\psi(s^{\prime})\right\rangle\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 6H^{2}\sqrt{d\lambda}\left\|\phi(s,i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}`$ |  | (115c) |

Here eq.
([115c](#A3.E115.3 "In 115 ‣ C.4.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from Lemma
([C.7](#A3.Thmlemma7 "Lemma C.7 (Range of Q, V functions). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and Lemma
([C.5](#A3.Thmlemma5 "Lemma C.5. ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
Using the equations
([115a](#A3.E115.1 "In 115 ‣ C.4.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),([115b](#A3.E115.2 "In 115 ‣ C.4.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
([115c](#A3.E115.3 "In 115 ‣ C.4.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
and the fact
$`\left\langle\phi(s,i,j),\theta^{+}_{h,t}\right\rangle-Q_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}(s,i,j)=\left\langle\phi(s,i,j),\theta^{+}_{h,t}-\theta_{h}^{\pi^{\star}}\right\rangle=\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{4}\rangle+\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{5}\rangle+\langle\phi(s,i,j),p_{6}\rangle`$
for $`\lambda=1`$, using similar arguments to Lemma
[C.1](#A3.Thmlemma1 "Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\langle\theta^{+}_{h,t},\phi(s,i,j)\rangle-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}V^{+}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\right|\leq c^{\prime}dH^{2}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)+\log\left(1+c_{2}+2c_{1}\right)}\left\|\phi(s,i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}`$ |  |

for some universal constant $`c^{\prime}`$ which is independent of
$`c_{1},c_{2}`$. Since $`dT/\delta>1`$ and $`c_{1}`$ is a fixed
universal constant from Lemma
[C.1](#A3.Thmlemma1 "Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
choosing a large enough $`c_{2}>c^{\prime}`$ we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\langle\theta^{+}_{h,t},\phi(s,i,j)\rangle-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}V^{+}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\right|\leq c_{2}dH^{2}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{16dTH}{\delta\min\{1,\beta\}}\right)}\left\|\phi(s,i,j)\right\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}.`$ |  |

This completes the proof of Lemma
[C.2](#A3.Thmlemma2 "Lemma C.2 (Concentration of Superoptimistic Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").

#### C.4.3 Proof of Corollary [C.1](#A3.Thmcorollary1 "Corollary C.1 (Bounds on Superoptimistic Bellman error w.r.t. the 𝑄^+ function). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")

From the definition of
$`Q_{h,t}^{+}(s,i,j)=\Pi^{+}_{h}\left(\langle\theta^{+}_{h,t},\phi(s,i,j)\rangle+b^{\texttt{sup}}_{h,t}(s,i,j)\right)`$,
under event $`\mathcal{E}_{7}`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|Q_{h,t}^{+}(s,i,j)-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}V^{+}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle=\left|\Pi^{+}_{h}\left(\langle\theta^{+}_{h,t},\phi(s,i,j)\rangle+b^{\texttt{sup}}_{h,t}(s,i,j)\right)-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}V^{+}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\left|\langle\theta^{+}_{h,t},\phi(s,i,j)\rangle+b^{\texttt{sup}}_{h,t}(s,i,j)-r_{h}(s,i,j)-P_{h}V^{+}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\right|`$ |  | (116) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq b^{\texttt{sup}}_{h,t}(s,i,j)+b_{h,t}(s,i,j)=2b_{h,t}(s,i,j)+2b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h,t}(s,i,j)`$ |  | (117) |

Here eq.
([116](#A3.E116 "In C.4.3 Proof of Corollary ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows since
$`r_{h}(s,i,j)+P_{h}V^{+}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\in[0,3(H-h+1)^{2}]`$ (Lemma
[C.7](#A3.Thmlemma7 "Lemma C.7 (Range of Q, V functions). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and the projection operator $`\Pi_{h}^{+}`$ whose output
$`\Pi_{h}^{+}(\cdot)\in[0,3(H-h+1)^{2}]`$ is a non-expansive map. Eq.
([117](#A3.E117 "In C.4.3 Proof of Corollary ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from Lemma
[C.2](#A3.Thmlemma2 "Lemma C.2 (Concentration of Superoptimistic Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
This concludes the proof.

#### C.4.4 Proof of Lemma [C.3](#A3.Thmlemma3 "Lemma C.3 (Optimism). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")

Firstly we note that whenever
$`Q^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})=3(H-h+1)^{2}`$ attains the maximum
possible clipped value, the lemma holds trivially since
$`Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\leq(H-h+1)^{2}`$ (from Lemma
[C.7](#A3.Thmlemma7 "Lemma C.7 (Range of Q, V functions). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and $`\overline{Q}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\leq H-h+1`$ (from the design
of the projection operator
([21a](#S3.E21.1 "In 21 ‣ 1st item ‣ 3.2 Algorithm Development ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))).
By convention, we know eq.
([74a](#A3.E74.1 "In 74 ‣ Lemma C.3 (Optimism). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
holds trivially when $`h=H+1`$ Assume the statement is true for $`h+1`$,
then under $`\mathcal{E}_{6}\cap\mathcal{E}_{7}`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle Q^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-\overline{Q}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:b_relations}}{=}\langle\theta^{+}_{h},\phi(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\rangle-r_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-P_{h}V^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+b_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+2b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+P_{h}\left(V^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-\overline{V}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\right)-\overline{e}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+P_{h}\left(V^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-\overline{V}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\right)`$ |  | (118) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}|s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1}))\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}|s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\mu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\mu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1}))\right)`$ |  | (119) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}|s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\mu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]\right)\geq 0,`$ |  | (120) |

where $`\overline{e}_{h}`$ is defined in
([72](#A3.E72 "In Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
eq.
([118](#A3.E118 "In C.4.4 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from Lemma
[C.1](#A3.Thmlemma1 "Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and Lemma
[C.2](#A3.Thmlemma2 "Lemma C.2 (Concentration of Superoptimistic Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
we omit the KL terms corresponding to the min player policy
$`(\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1}))`$ since it is the same for both
$`V_{h+1}^{+}`$ and $`\overline{V}_{h+1}`$ in eq.
([119](#A3.E119 "In C.4.4 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
and we swap $`\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})`$ by
$`\mu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})`$ in the first term of eq.
([120](#A3.E120 "In C.4.4 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and the inequality follows from the optimality of the superoptimistic
best response policy $`\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})`$ under
$`Q^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},\cdot,\cdot)`$ and $`\nu_{h+1}`$, and the
induction hypothesis gives the last inequality. Using similar arguments,
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle Q^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-Q^{\mu^{\prime}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\langle\theta^{+}_{h},\phi(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\rangle-r_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-P_{h}V^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+b_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+2b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+P_{h}\left(V^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-V^{\mu^{\prime}}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq 2b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}|s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1}))\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}|s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\mu^{\prime}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{\mu^{\prime}}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\mu^{\prime}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1}))\right)`$ |  | (121) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq 2b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}|s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\mu^{\prime}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-Q^{\mu^{\prime}}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]\right)\geq 0.`$ |  | (122) |

Here eq.
([121](#A3.E121 "In C.4.4 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from Lemma
[C.2](#A3.Thmlemma2 "Lemma C.2 (Concentration of Superoptimistic Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
Eq.
([122](#A3.E122 "In C.4.4 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from the optimality of the superoptimistic best response policy
$`\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})`$ under
$`Q^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},\cdot,\cdot)`$ and $`\nu_{h+1}`$ and the induction
hypothesis implies the penultimate expression is positive.

#### C.4.5 Proof of Lemma [C.4](#A3.Thmlemma4 "Lemma C.4 (Super-optimistic gap). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")

From Lemma
[C.3](#A3.Thmlemma3 "Lemma C.3 (Optimism). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
we have
$`Q^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\geq\overline{Q}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$
and $`Q^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\geq Q^{\mu}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$.
Note that whenever we have an underestimate of $`Q^{\mu}`$, i.e,
$`Q^{\mu}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\geq\overline{Q}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$
we have eq.
([75](#A3.E75 "In Lemma C.4 (Super-optimistic gap). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
hold automatically even without the 2x multiplier hence we will only
concern ourselves with the case where we overestimate $`Q^{\mu}`$, i.e.,
$`Q^{\mu}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\leq\overline{Q}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$.
We also note that when $`Q^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})=3(H-h+1)^{2}`$
attains the maximum possible clipped value the statement holds trivially
again since $`\overline{Q}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\leq(H-h+1)`$ (from the
design of the projection operator
([21a](#S3.E21.1 "In 21 ‣ 1st item ‣ 3.2 Algorithm Development ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")))
and
$`Q^{\mu}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\geq-(H-h+1)^{2}\;\forall\;(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$
(from Lemma
[C.7](#A3.Thmlemma7 "Lemma C.7 (Range of Q, V functions). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
Since (by Lemma
[C.2](#A3.Thmlemma2 "Lemma C.2 (Concentration of Superoptimistic Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\langle\theta^{+}_{h},\phi(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\rangle+b^{\texttt{sup}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\geq r_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+P_{h}V_{h+1}^{+}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+2b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})>0,
``` |  |

we only need to prove the equation in the overestimation case where

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
0<Q^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})=\langle\theta^{+}_{h,t},\phi(s,i,j)\rangle+b^{\texttt{sup}}_{h,t}(s,i,j)<3(H-h+1)^{2},
``` |  |

where eq.
([75](#A3.E75 "In Lemma C.4 (Super-optimistic gap). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
(by Lemma
[C.3](#A3.Thmlemma3 "Lemma C.3 (Optimism). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
is equivalent to

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
Q^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-\overline{Q}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\geq\overline{Q}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-Q_{h}^{\mu}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}),
``` |  |

which we do via an induction argument. We know that eq.
([75](#A3.E75 "In Lemma C.4 (Super-optimistic gap). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
holds trivially for $`h=H+1`$. Assume it holds for $`h+1`$. We will show
that it also holds for $`h`$.

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle Q^{+}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-\overline{Q}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\langle\theta^{+}_{h},\phi(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\rangle-r_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-P_{h}V^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+b_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+2b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+P_{h}\left(V^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-\overline{V}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\right)-\overline{e}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+P_{h}\left(V^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-\overline{V}_{h+1}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\right)`$ |  | (123) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}|s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1}))\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}|s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\mu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\mu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1}))\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}|s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\mu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]\right)`$ |  | (124) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}|s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}}\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{h+1}\sim\mu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\\
j_{h+1}\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})\end{subarray}}\left[\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})-Q^{\mu}_{h+1}(s_{h+1},i_{h+1},j_{h+1})\right]\right)`$ |  | (125) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1}|s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}}(\overline{V}_{h+1}(s_{h+1})-V^{\mu}_{h+1}(s_{h+1}))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=b^{\texttt{mse}}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})+\overline{Q}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-Q^{\mu}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-\overline{e}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\overline{Q}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-Q^{\mu}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}).`$ |  | (126) |

Here eq.
([123](#A3.E123 "In C.4.5 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from Lemma
[C.1](#A3.Thmlemma1 "Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and Lemma
[C.2](#A3.Thmlemma2 "Lemma C.2 (Concentration of Superoptimistic Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
Eq.
([124](#A3.E124 "In C.4.5 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
swaps $`\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})`$ by
$`\mu_{h+1}(\cdot|s_{h+1})`$ in the first term and the inequality
follows since the optimality of policy $`\tilde{\mu}(\cdot|s_{h+1})`$
under $`Q^{+}(s_{h+1},\cdot,\cdot)`$ and eq.
([125](#A3.E125 "In C.4.5 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from the induction hypothesis
$`\left(2\left|\left(Q^{+}_{h+1}(s,i,j)-\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\right)\right|\geq\left|Q^{+}_{h+1}(s,i,j)-Q_{h+1}^{\mu}(s,i,j)\right|\right)`$
alongside the optimism lemma (Lemma
[C.3](#A3.Thmlemma3 "Lemma C.3 (Optimism). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
implies
$`Q^{+}_{h+1}(s,i,j)-\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s,i,j)\geq\overline{Q}_{h+1}(s,i,j)-Q_{h+1}^{\mu}(s,i,j)`$.
Eq.
([126](#A3.E126 "In C.4.5 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.4 Proofs of Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from Lemma
[C.1](#A3.Thmlemma1 "Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").

### C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas

###### Lemma C.5.

If
$`(\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}):=(\mu^{\prime}_{h},\nu^{\prime}_{h})_{h=1}^{H}`$
is the Nash Equilibrium of a KL regularized Markov Game where
$`0\leq r^{\prime}_{h}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})\leq 1`$. Let
$`V_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s):=\mathbb{E}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}\left[\sum_{k=h}^{H}r^{\prime}_{k}(s_{k},i,j)-\beta\log\frac{\mu^{\prime}_{k}(i|s_{k})}{\mu_{\text{ref},k}(i|s_{k})}+\beta\log\frac{\nu^{\prime}_{k}(j|s_{k})}{\nu_{\text{ref},k}(j|s_{k})}\middle|s_{h}=s\right]`$
and
$`Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s,i,j):=r^{\prime}_{h}(s,i,j)+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}s^{\prime}\sim P_{h}(\cdot|s,i,j)\end{subarray}}\left[V_{h+1}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s^{\prime})\right]`$
be the value and Q functions under this game. Then
$`\forall(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}},h\in[H]`$,
$`\beta>0`$ we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i,j)`$ | $`\displaystyle\in[0,H-h+1],`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h})`$ | $`\displaystyle\in[0,H-h+1],`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\beta\text{KL}\left(\mu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|s_{h})\right)`$ | $`\displaystyle\in[0,H-h+1],`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\beta\text{KL}\left(\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})\|\nu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|s_{h})\right)`$ | $`\displaystyle\in[0,H-h+1].`$ |  |

Proof. We prove the proposition using induction. The statement is true
trivially for $`h=H+1`$. Assume the statement is true for $`h+1`$ then
we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i,j)=r^{\prime}_{h}(s_{h},i,j)+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}s^{\prime}\sim P_{h}(\cdot|s_{h},i,j)\end{subarray}}\left[V^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime})\right].`$ |  |

Since $`V^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime})\in[0,H-h]`$ and
$`r^{\prime}_{h}(s_{h},i,j)\in[0,1]`$, we have
$`Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i,j)\in[0,H-h+1]`$. In
addition,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h})=\;\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\\[2.0pt]
j\sim\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\end{subarray}}\!\left[Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i,j)\right]-\beta\text{KL}\left(\mu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})\|\mu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot|s_{h})\right)+\beta\text{KL}\left(\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})\|\nu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot|s_{h})\right).`$ |  |

Using the closed form expression for
$`\mu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})`$ (see eq.
([14](#S3.E14 "In 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")))
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\beta\log\left(\sum_{i}\mu_{\text{ref},h}(i|s_{h})\exp\left(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{j\sim\nu^{\prime}(\cdot\mid s_{h})}\left[Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i,j)\right]/\beta\right)\right)+\beta\text{KL}\left(\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})\|\nu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|s_{h})\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\\
j\sim\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i,j)\right]+\beta\text{KL}\left(\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})\|\nu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|s_{h})\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq 0.`$ |  |

Here the second line uses
$`\log\left(\mathbb{E}[X]\right)\geq\mathbb{E}\left[\log(X)\right]`$
(Jensen’s inequality). Similarly, using the closed form expression for
$`\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})`$ (see eq.
([15](#S3.E15 "In 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")))
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h})`$ | $`\displaystyle=-\beta\log\left(\sum_{j}\nu_{\text{ref},h}(i|s_{h})\exp\left(-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{i\sim\mu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})}\left[Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i,j)\right]/\beta\right)\right)-\beta\text{KL}\left(\mu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|s_{h})\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu^{\prime}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\\
j\sim\nu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i,j)\right]-\beta\text{KL}\left(\mu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|s_{h})\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq H-h+1.`$ |  |

Lastly, note that since $`\mu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})`$ is the Nash
equilibrium point, for a fixed $`\nu^{\prime}_{h}`$ we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\\[2.0pt]
j\sim\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\end{subarray}}\!\left[Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i,j)\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\mu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|s_{h}))\geq\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\\[2.0pt]
j\sim\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\end{subarray}}\!\left[Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i,j)\right],`$ |  |

which gives

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
````
