````math
|  | $`\displaystyle\beta\text{KL}(\mu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|s_{h}))\leq\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\\[2.0pt]
j\sim\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\end{subarray}}\!\left[Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i,j)\right]-\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\\[2.0pt]
j\sim\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot\mid s_{h})\end{subarray}}\!\left[Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime}}(s_{h},i,j)\right]\leq H-h+1.`$ |  |

Similar argument using the min player can be used to obtain
$`\beta\text{KL}\left(\nu^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})\|\nu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|s_{h})\right)\in[0,H-h+1]`$.
 

###### Lemma C.6.

Let $`(\mu_{t},\nu_{t}):=(\mu_{h,t},\nu_{h,t})_{h=1}^{H}`$ be the
estimated stagewise Nash Equilibrium policies of a KL regularized Matrix
Game as defined in eq.
([18](#S3.E18 "In 7 ‣ Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
of Algorithm
[2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
Then
$`\forall(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}},h\in[H]`$,
$`\beta>0`$, we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}_{h,t}(s_{h},i,j)`$ | $`\displaystyle\in[0,H-h+1],`$ |  | (127a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}_{h,t}(s_{h})`$ | $`\displaystyle\in[0,H-h+1],`$ |  | (127b) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\beta\text{KL}\left(\mu_{h,t}(\cdot|s_{h})\|\mu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|s_{h})\right)`$ | $`\displaystyle\in[0,H-h+1],`$ |  | (127c) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\beta\text{KL}\left(\nu_{h,t}(\cdot|s_{h})\|\nu_{\text{ref},h}(\cdot|s_{h})\right)`$ | $`\displaystyle\in[0,H-h+1].`$ |  | (127d) |

Proof. We know $`\overline{Q}_{h,t}(s_{h},i,j)\in[0,H-h+1]`$ by the
design of the projection operator $`\Pi_{h}`$. And since

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
(\mu_{h,t}(\cdot|s),\nu_{h,t}(\cdot|s))\leftarrow\text{KL reg Nash Zero-sum}(\overline{Q}_{h,t}(s,\cdot,\cdot)),
``` |  |

using the same arguments as Lemma
[C.5](#A3.Thmlemma5 "Lemma C.5. ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
one can prove equations
([127b](#A3.E127.2 "In 127 ‣ Lemma C.6. ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))-([127d](#A3.E127.4 "In 127 ‣ Lemma C.6. ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
 

The next lemma provides upper and lower bounds on the functions $`Q`$
and $`V`$, which will be used in our analysis. We provide loose bounds
on some of these terms for simplicity.

###### Lemma C.7 (Range of Q, V functions).

Under the setting in Algorithm
[2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
for any $`t\in[T]`$, we have the following ranges for the Bellman
target, value and $`Q`$ functions for all
$`\forall(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}},h\in[H]`$
and $`\beta>0`$:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h+1,t}^{+}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle\in[0,3(H-h)^{2}+(H-h)],`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle r_{h}(s,i,j)+P_{h}V_{h+1,t}^{+}(s,i,j)`$ | $`\displaystyle\in[0,3(H-h+1)^{2}],`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle Q_{h}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s,i,j)`$ | $`\displaystyle\in[-(H-h+1)^{2},(H-h+1)^{2}],`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle\in[-(H-h+1)^{2},(H-h+1)^{2}+(H-h+1)].`$ |  |

We also have for any policy $`\mu^{\prime}`$:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu_{t}}(s,i,j)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq(H-h+1)^{2},`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu_{t}}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq(H-h+1)^{2}+(H-h+1).`$ |  |

Proof. Here we omit the subscript $`t`$ for notational simplicity while
proving the first two statements. We have
$`Q_{h+1}^{+}(s,i,j)\in[0,3(H-h)^{2}]`$,
$`\forall(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}},h\in[H]`$
by definition of the projection operator $`\Pi_{h}^{+}`$ (see eq.
([21b](#S3.E21.2 "In 21 ‣ 1st item ‣ 3.2 Algorithm Development ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))).
We have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h+1}^{+}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{h+1}^{+}(s,i,j)\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s)||\mu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s))+\beta\text{KL}(\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s)||\nu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s))`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{h+1}^{+}(s,i,j)\right]+\beta\text{KL}(\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s)||\nu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s))\leq 3(H-h)^{2}+(H-h),`$ |  | (128) |

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
[C.6](#A3.Thmlemma6 "Lemma C.6. ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and
([21](#S3.E21 "In 1st item ‣ 3.2 Algorithm Development ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
Thus
$`\forall(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}},h\in[H]`$
we also have the target for the Bellman update

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
r_{h}(s,i,j)+P_{h}V_{h+1,t}^{+}(s,i,j)\leq 1+3(H-h)^{2}+(H-h)\leq 3(H-h+1)^{2},
``` |  |

and

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h+1}^{+}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{h+1}^{+}(s,i,j)\right]-\beta\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1}(\cdot|s)||\mu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s))+\beta\text{KL}(\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s)||\nu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s))`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{h+1}^{+}(s,i,j)\right]+\beta\text{KL}(\nu_{h+1}(\cdot|s)||\nu_{\text{ref},h+1}(\cdot|s))\geq 0.`$ |  |

Therefore,
$`\forall(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V}},h\in[H]`$,
we have

|     |                                               |     |
|-----|-----------------------------------------------|-----|
|     |
       ``` math
       r_{h}(s,i,j)+P_{h}V_{h+1,t}^{+}(s,i,j)\geq 0.
       ```                                            |     |

One can rewrite eq.
([10](#S3.E10 "In 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
at step $`h+1`$ as

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h+1}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu_{t}}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu_{t}}\left[\sum_{k=h+1}^{H}r_{k}(s_{k},i,j)-\beta\text{KL}\left(\mu^{\prime}_{k}(\cdot|s_{k})\|\mu_{\text{ref},k}(\cdot|s_{k})\right)+\beta\text{KL}\left(\nu_{k,t}(\cdot|s_{k})\|\nu_{\text{ref},k}(\cdot|s_{k})\right)\middle|s_{h}=s\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu_{t}}\left[\sum_{k=h+1}^{H}r_{k}(s_{k},i,j)+\beta\text{KL}\left(\nu_{k,t}(\cdot|s_{k})\|\nu_{\text{ref},k}(\cdot|s_{k})\right)\middle|s_{h}=s\right]\leq(H-h)^{2}+(H-h),`$ |  | (129a) |
| where the last inequality is due to Lemma [C.6](#A3.Thmlemma6 "Lemma C.6. ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"). Thus for any policy $`\mu^{\prime}`$ we have |  |  |  |  |
|  |
``` math
Q_{h}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu_{t}}(s,i,j)=r_{h}(s,i,j)+P_{h}V_{h+1}^{\mu^{\prime},\nu_{t}}(s,i,j)\leq(H-h+1)^{2}.
``` |  |  |  |
| Similarly, we have for any $`s\in\mathcal{S},\;h\in[H]`$: |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h+1}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\sum_{k=h+1}^{H}r_{k}(s_{k},i,j)-\beta\text{KL}\left(\mu_{k,t}(\cdot|s_{k})\|\mu_{\text{ref},k}(\cdot|s_{k})\right)+\beta\text{KL}\left(\nu_{k,t}(\cdot|s_{k})\|\nu_{\text{ref},k}(\cdot|s_{k})\right)\middle|s_{h}=s\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}\left[\sum_{k=h+1}^{H}-\beta\text{KL}\left(\mu_{k,t}(\cdot|s_{k})\|\mu_{\text{ref},k}(\cdot|s_{k})\right)\middle|s_{h}=s\right]\geq-(H-h)^{2}.`$ |  | (129b) |

Since

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
Q_{h}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s,i,j)=r_{h}(s,i,j)+P_{h}V_{h+1}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s,i,j)
``` |  |

and $`r_{h}(s,i,j)\in[0,1]`$, using
([129a](#A3.E129.1 "In 129 ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and
([129b](#A3.E129.2 "In 129 ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
we have

|     |                                                              |     |
|-----|--------------------------------------------------------------|-----|
|     |
       ``` math
       Q_{h}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s,i,j)\in[-(H-h+1)^{2},(H-h+1)^{2}].
       ```                                                           |     |

 

This following lemma is a consequence of the linear MDP, similar results
can be found in [Jin et al. \[2020\]](#bib.bib4) (Lemma B.1) and [Xie et
al. \[2023\]](#bib.bib11) (Lemma 7).

###### Lemma C.8 (Linearity of the Q function).

Let $`(\mu_{t},\nu_{t}):=(\mu_{h,t},\nu_{h,t})_{h=1}^{H}`$ be the
estimated stagewise Nash Equilibrium policies as defined in eq.
([18](#S3.E18 "In 7 ‣ Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
of Algorithm
[2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
then under the linear MDP (Assumption
[3](#Thmassumption3 "Assumption 3 (Linear MDP ( ) ). ‣ 3.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
there exist weights $`\{\theta_{h}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}\}_{h=1}^{H}`$ such
that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
Q_{h}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s,i,j)=\langle\phi(s,i,j),\theta_{h}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}\rangle\qquad\mathrm{and}\qquad\left\|\theta_{h}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}\right\|\leq 3H^{2}\sqrt{d},\qquad\forall(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V},h\in[H].
``` |  |

Similarly for the Nash equilibrium policy
$`(\mu^{\star},\nu^{\star})=(\mu^{\star}_{h},\nu^{\star}_{h})_{h=1}^{H}`$
then there exist weights
$`\{\theta^{\mu^{\star},\nu^{\star}}_{h}\}_{h=1}^{H}`$ such that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
Q_{h}^{\mu^{\star},\nu^{\star}}(s,i,j)=\left\langle\phi(s,i,j),\theta_{h}^{\mu^{\star},\nu^{\star}}\right\rangle\qquad\mathrm{and}\qquad\left\|\theta_{h}^{\mu^{\star},\nu^{\star}}\right\|\leq 2H\sqrt{d},\qquad\forall(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{U}\times\mathcal{V},h\in[H].
``` |  |

Proof. From the Bellman eq.
([11](#S3.E11 "In 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle Q_{h}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s,i,j):=r_{h}(s,i,j)+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}s^{\prime}\sim P_{h}(\cdot|s_{h},i,j)\end{subarray}}\left[V_{h+1}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s^{\prime})\right].`$ |  |

From the definition of linear MDP (c.f.
Assumption [3](#Thmassumption3 "Assumption 3 (Linear MDP ( ) ). ‣ 3.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we know that can set

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\theta_{h}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}=\omega_{h}+\int V_{h+1}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s^{\prime})d\psi(s^{\prime})\leq 3H^{2}\sqrt{d}.
``` |  |

since $`\|\omega_{h}\|\leq\sqrt{d}`$ and
$`\left\|\int V_{h+1}^{\mu_{t},\nu_{t}}(s^{\prime})d\psi(s^{\prime})\right\|\leq 2H^{2}\sqrt{d}`$
(from Lemma
[C.7](#A3.Thmlemma7 "Lemma C.7 (Range of Q, V functions). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
Similarly, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\theta_{h}^{\mu^{\star},\nu^{\star}}=\omega_{h}+\int V_{h+1}^{\mu^{\star},\nu^{\star}}(s^{\prime})d\psi(s^{\prime}).`$ |  | (130) |

Using
$`\left\|\int V_{h+1}^{\mu^{\star},\nu^{\star}}(s^{\prime})d\psi(s^{\prime})\right\|\leq H\sqrt{d}`$
(from Lemma
[C.5](#A3.Thmlemma5 "Lemma C.5. ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have $`\|\theta_{h}^{\mu^{\star},\nu^{\star}}\|\leq 2H\sqrt{d}.`$  

Note that the proof of Proposition
[3.1](#S3.Thmproposition1 "Proposition 3.1. ‣ 3.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
is contained in the proofs of Lemma
[C.5](#A3.Thmlemma5 "Lemma C.5. ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and
[C.8](#A3.Thmlemma8 "Lemma C.8 (Linearity of the Q function). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
The following lemma bounds the $`L_{2}`$ norms of the estimated
parameters ($`\overline{\theta}_{h,t}`$ and $`\theta^{+}_{h,t}`$) and is
similar to [Jin et al. \[2020\]](#bib.bib4) (Lemma B.2) and [Xie et al.
\[2023\]](#bib.bib11) (Lemma 8).

###### Lemma C.9 ($`L_{2}`$ norm bounds).

For all $`h\in[H],t\in[T]`$, we have the following bounds on the
$`L_{2}`$ norms:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\|\overline{\theta}_{h,t}\|`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq 2H\sqrt{2dt/\lambda}\quad\text{and}\quad\|\theta^{+}_{h,t}\|\leq 4H^{2}\sqrt{2dt/\lambda}.`$ |  |

Proof. We have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\max_{\|\mathbf{x}\|=1}\left|\mathbf{x}^{\top}\overline{\theta}_{h,t}\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle=\left|\mathbf{x}^{\top}\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}\left[r_{h,\tau}+\overline{V}_{h+1,t}(s^{\tau}_{h+1})\right]\right|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2H\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\left|\mathbf{x}^{\top}\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\phi_{h,\tau}\right|\leq 2H\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\left|\mathbf{x}\right|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}\left|\phi_{h,\tau}\right|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2H\sqrt{\left[\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\mathbf{x}^{\top}\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\mathbf{x}\right]\left[\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}^{\top}\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\phi_{h,\tau}\right]}\leq 2H\sqrt{2dt/\lambda}.`$ |  |

where the first inequality follows from Lemma
[C.6](#A3.Thmlemma6 "Lemma C.6. ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and the last inequality follows from Lemma
[A.7](#A1.Thmlemma7 "Lemma A.7 (Lemma D.1 in [ ] ). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
Similarly, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\max_{\|\mathbf{x}\|=1}\left|\mathbf{x}^{\top}\theta^{+}_{h,t}\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle=\left|\mathbf{x}^{\top}\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}\left[r_{h,\tau}+V^{+}_{h+1,t}(s^{\tau}_{h+1})\right]\right|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 4H^{2}\sqrt{\left[\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\mathbf{x}^{\top}\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\mathbf{x}\right]\left[\sum_{\tau\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}\phi_{h,\tau}^{\top}\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}\phi_{h,\tau}\right]}\leq 4H^{2}\sqrt{2dt/\lambda}.`$ |  |

here the first inequality follows from Lemma
[C.7](#A3.Thmlemma7 "Lemma C.7 (Range of Q, V functions). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and the last inequality follows from Lemma
[A.7](#A1.Thmlemma7 "Lemma A.7 (Lemma D.1 in [ ] ). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
 

The following lemma provides an upper bound on the covering number of
the value functions induced by the $`Q`$-function estimates in
Algorithm [2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
when $`\beta>0`$. The original result for the unregularized setting
appears in [Jin et al. \[2020\]](#bib.bib4) (Lemma D.6).

###### Lemma C.10 (Covering number of the MSE Value function class in Algorithm [2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).

For some $`\beta>0`$, let $`\mathcal{V}^{\texttt{mse}}`$ denote the
function class on the state space $`\mathcal{S}`$ with the parametric
form

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V(s)\coloneqq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{Q}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{Q}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q(s,i,j)\right]-\beta\text{KL}\left(\mu_{Q}(\cdot|s)\|\mu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot|s)\right)+\beta\text{KL}\left(\nu_{Q}(\cdot|s)\|\nu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot|s)\right),`$ |  |

for fixed policies $`\nu_{\text{ref}},\mu_{\text{ref}}`$ (we omit the
index $`h\in[H]`$), where $`Q(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{Q}(s,i,j)`$ and
$`\mu_{Q}(\cdot|s)`$ and $`\nu_{Q}(\cdot|s)`$ being the Nash equilibrium
policies for the KL regularized game with the payoff matrix
$`Q(s,\cdot,\cdot)`$. $`\mathcal{Q}`$ is a function class on the space
$`\mathcal{S\times U\times V}`$ with the parametric form

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle Q(s,i,j)=\Pi_{(b_{2},B_{2})}\left(\boldsymbol{\theta}^{\top}\phi(s,i,j)+\eta\sqrt{\phi(s,i,j)^{\top}\mathbf{\Sigma^{-1}}\phi(s,i,j)}\right)`$ |  |

with function parameters $`\|\boldsymbol{\theta}\|\leq L`$,
$`\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{\Sigma})\geq\lambda`$ and
$`0\leq\eta\leq B_{3}`$, and we define
$`\Pi_{(b_{2},B_{2})}(\cdot)=\min\{\max\{\cdot,b_{2}\},B_{2}\}`$ where
$`b_{2}\leq B_{2}`$ are function class parameters (fixed for a given
$`h\in[H]`$). Then the covering number of the class
$`\mathcal{V}^{\texttt{mse}}`$ w.r.t the $`L_{\infty}`$-norm
$`\text{dist}(V_{1},V_{2})=\sup_{s}|V_{1}(s)-V_{2}(s)|`$ can be upper
bounded as

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\log\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{mse}}_{\varepsilon}\leq d\log(1+4L/\varepsilon)+d^{2}\log\bigl(1+8d^{1/2}B_{3}^{2}/(\lambda\varepsilon^{2})\bigr).`$ |  | (131) |

Proof. We can reparameterize any function $`Q\in\mathcal{Q}`$ as
follows:

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle Q(s,i,j)=\Pi_{(b_{2},B_{2})}\left(\boldsymbol{\theta}^{\top}\phi(s,i,j)+\sqrt{\phi(s,i,j)^{\top}\mathbf{A}\phi(s,i,j)}\right),`$ |  |

for the positive semi-definite matrix
$`\mathbf{A}=\eta^{2}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{-1}`$ with the spectral norm
$`\|\mathbf{A}\|\leq B_{3}^{2}/\lambda`$ (which implies
$`\|\mathbf{A}\|_{F}\leq d^{1/2}B_{3}^{2}/\lambda`$ ) Let
$`V_{1}(\cdot)`$ and $`V_{2}(\cdot)`$ be the value functions induced by
$`Q_{1}(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot)`$ (parameterized by
$`\boldsymbol{\theta_{1}}`$, $`\mathbf{A}_{1}`$, induced policies
$`\mu_{1},\nu_{1}`$) and $`Q_{2}(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot)`$ (parameterized by
$`\boldsymbol{\theta_{2}}`$, $`\mathbf{A_{2}}`$, induced policies
$`\mu_{2},\nu_{2}`$) respectively. Define

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{1}(s,\mu,\nu):=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{1}(s,i,j)\right]-\beta\text{KL}\left(\mu(\cdot|s)\|\mu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot|s)\right)+\beta\text{KL}\left(\nu(\cdot|s)\|\nu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot|s)\right),`$ |  |

then we have $`V_{1}(s,\mu_{1},\nu_{1})=V_{1}(s)`$, similarly define
$`V_{2}(s,\mu,\nu)`$, then we have  

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\text{dist}(V_{1},V_{2})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sup_{s}|V_{1}(s)-V_{2}(s)|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sup_{s}|V_{1}(s,\mu_{1},\nu_{1})-V_{2}(s,\mu_{2},\nu_{2})|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sup_{s}\max\left\{V_{1}(s,\mu_{1},\nu_{1})-V_{2}(s,\mu_{2},\nu_{2}),V_{2}(s,\mu_{2},\nu_{2})-V_{1}(s,\mu_{1},\nu_{1})\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sup_{s}\max\left\{|V_{1}(s,\mu_{1},\nu_{2})-V_{2}(s,\mu_{1},\nu_{2})|,|V_{2}(s,\mu_{2},\nu_{1})-V_{1}(s,\mu_{2},\nu_{1})|\right\}`$ |  | (132) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sup_{s}\max\left\{\left|\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{1}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{2}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{1}(s,i,j)-Q_{2}(s,i,j)\right]\right|,\left|\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu_{2}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{1}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q_{2}(s,i,j)-Q_{1}(s,i,j)\right]\right|\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sup_{s,i,j}\left|Q_{1}(s,i,j)-Q_{2}(s,i,j)\right|`$ |  | (133) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\|\phi\|\leq 1}\left|\left(\boldsymbol{\theta_{1}}^{\top}\phi+\sqrt{\phi^{\top}\mathbf{A_{1}}\phi}\right)-\left(\boldsymbol{\theta_{2}}^{\top}\phi+\sqrt{\phi^{\top}\mathbf{A_{2}}\phi}\right)\right|`$ |  | (134) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{1}-\boldsymbol{\theta_{2}}\|+\sqrt{\|\mathbf{A_{1}}-\textbf{A}_{2}\|}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{\theta_{1}}-\boldsymbol{\theta_{2}}\|+\sqrt{\|\mathbf{A_{1}}-\mathbf{A_{2}}\|_{F}},`$ |  |  |

where eq.
([132](#A3.E132 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from the fact
$`V_{1}(s,\mu_{1},\nu_{1})\leq V_{1}(s,\mu_{1},\nu^{\prime})\;\forall\nu^{\prime}`$
and
$`V_{2}(s,\mu_{2},\nu_{2})\geq V_{2}(s,\mu^{\prime},\nu_{2})\;\forall\mu^{\prime}`$
from the properties of Nash equilibrium resulting in

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{1}(s,\mu_{1},\nu_{1})-V_{2}(s,\mu_{2},\nu_{2})\leq V_{1}(s,\mu_{1},\nu_{2})-V_{2}(s,\mu_{1},\nu_{2})\leq|V_{1}(s,\mu_{1},\nu_{2})-V_{2}(s,\mu_{1},\nu_{2})|,`$ |  |

when $`V_{1}(s)\geq V_{2}(s)`$. The other term is similarly obtained
when $`V_{1}(s)\leq V_{2}(s)`$ and eq.
([134](#A3.E134 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows since
$`\Pi_{(b_{2},B_{2})}(\cdot)=\min\{\max\{\cdot,b_{2}\},B_{2}\}`$ is
non-expansive, the penultimate line uses the fact

|     |                                      |     |
|-----|--------------------------------------|-----|
|     |
       ``` math
       |\sqrt{x}-\sqrt{y}|\leq\sqrt{|x-y|},
       ```                                   |     |

giving us

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sup_{\|\phi\|\leq 1}\left|\sqrt{\phi^{\top}\mathbf{A_{1}}\phi}-\sqrt{\phi^{\top}\mathbf{A_{2}}\phi}\right|\leq\sup_{\|\phi\|\leq 1}\sqrt{|\phi^{\top}(\mathbf{A_{1}}-\mathbf{A_{2}})\phi|}\leq\sqrt{\|\mathbf{A_{1}}-\mathbf{A_{2}}\|}.
``` |  |

Applying Lemma
[A.1](#A1.Thmlemma1 "Lemma A.1 (Covering number of the ℓ_2 ball, Lemma D.5 in [ ] ). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
to upper bound the cardinality of the $`\mathcal{C}_{\theta}:`$ the
$`\varepsilon/2`$ cover of
$`\left\{\boldsymbol{\theta}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}|\|\boldsymbol{\theta}\|\leq L\right\}`$
and $`\mathcal{C}_{A}:`$ the $`\varepsilon^{2}/4`$ cover of
$`\{\mathbf{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}\mid\|\mathbf{A}\|_{F}\leq d^{1/2}B_{3}^{2}\lambda^{-1}\}`$
with respect to the Frobenius norm, we obtain

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\log\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}^{\texttt{mse}}\leq\log|\mathcal{C}_{\theta}|+\log|\mathcal{C}_{A}|\leq d\log(1+4L/\varepsilon)+d^{2}\log\bigl[1+8d^{1/2}B_{3}^{2}/(\lambda\varepsilon^{2})\bigr].`$ |  | (135) |

Note that for the MSE function class in algorithm
[2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
has $`\eta=0`$, we presented the loose bound above toreuse the steps in
a later proof.

###### Lemma C.11 (Covering number of the superoptimistic value function class in Algorithm [2](#alg2 "Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).

For some $`\beta>0`$, let $`\mathcal{V}^{\texttt{sup}}`$ denote the
function class on the state space $`\mathcal{S}`$ with the parametric
form

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V(s)\coloneqq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{\overline{Q}}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}(s,i,j)\right]-\beta\text{KL}\left(\tilde{\mu}(\cdot|s)\|\mu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot|s)\right)+\beta\text{KL}\left(\nu_{\overline{Q}}(\cdot|s)\|\nu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot|s)\right),`$ |  |

for fixed policies $`\nu_{\text{ref}},\mu_{\text{ref}}`$ (we omit the
index $`h\in[H]`$), where
$`\overline{Q}(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{\overline{Q}}(s,i,j)`$,
$`Q^{+}(s,i,j)\in\mathcal{Q^{+}}(s,i,j)`$,
$`\nu_{\overline{Q}}(\cdot|s)`$ is the min player KL regularized Nash
equilibrium policy for a payoff matrix $`\overline{Q}(s,\cdot,\cdot)`$
and $`\tilde{\mu}(\cdot|s)`$ is the max player’s KL regularized best
response to min player policy $`\nu_{\overline{Q}}(\cdot|s)`$ under the
payoff matrix $`Q^{+}(s,\cdot,\cdot)`$. Hence $`V`$ is fully
parameterized by $`Q^{+},\overline{Q}`$. The functions $`Q^{+}`$ and
$`\overline{Q}`$ have the following parametric forms:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}(s,i,j)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\Pi_{(b_{2},B_{2})}\left(\boldsymbol{\overline{\theta}}^{\top}\phi(s,i,j)\right),`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle Q^{+}(s,i,j)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\Pi_{(b_{2}^{+},B_{2}^{+})}\left(\left(\boldsymbol{\theta^{+}}\right)^{\top}\phi(s,i,j)+\eta\sqrt{\phi(s,i,j)^{\top}\mathbf{\Sigma^{-1}}\phi(s,i,j)}\right),`$ |  |

with function parameters $`\|\boldsymbol{\overline{\theta}}\|\leq L`$,
$`\|\boldsymbol{\theta^{+}}\|\leq L^{+}`$,
$`\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{\Sigma})\geq\lambda`$ and
$`0\leq\eta\leq B_{3}`$, and we define
$`\Pi_{(a,b)}(\cdot)=\min\{\max\{\cdot,a\},b\}`$ and
$`b_{2}\leq B_{2},\;b_{2}^{+}<B_{2}^{+}`$ are function class parameters
(fixed for a given $`h\in[H]`$). Then the covering number of the class
$`\mathcal{V}^{\texttt{sup}}`$ w.r.t the $`L_{\infty}`$-norm
$`\text{dist}(V_{1},V_{2})=\sup_{s}|V_{1}(s)-V_{2}(s)|`$ can be upper
bounded as

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\log\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}^{\texttt{sup}}\leq d\log(1+12L^{+}/\varepsilon)`$ | $`\displaystyle+d^{2}\log\bigl(1+72d^{1/2}(B_{3})^{2}/(\lambda\varepsilon^{2})\bigr)+d\log(1+24L/\varepsilon)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+d\log(1+144L(B_{2}^{+}+B_{2}-b_{2})^{2}/\varepsilon^{2}\beta)`$ |  |

Proof. We can reparameterize any function $`Q^{+}\in\mathcal{Q^{+}}`$ as
follows:

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle Q^{+}(s,i,j)=\Pi_{(b_{2}^{+},B_{2}^{+})}\left(\left(\boldsymbol{\theta^{+}}\right)^{\top}\phi(s,i,j)+\sqrt{\phi(s,i,j)^{\top}\mathbf{A}\phi(s,i,j)}\right),`$ |  |

for the positive semi-definite matrix $`A=\eta^{2}\Sigma^{-1}`$ with the
spectral norm $`\|\mathbf{A}\|\leq B_{3}^{2}/\lambda`$ (which implies
$`\|\mathbf{A}\|_{F}\leq d^{1/2}B_{3}^{2}/\lambda`$ ). Let
$`V_{1}(\cdot)`$ and $`V_{2}(\cdot)`$ be the value functions induced by
$`Q^{+}_{1}(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot)`$,
$`\overline{Q}_{1}(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot)`$ (parameterized by
$`\boldsymbol{\overline{\theta}_{1}},\boldsymbol{\theta^{+}_{1}}`$,
$`\mathbf{A_{1}}`$, induced policies $`\tilde{\mu}_{1}`$ and
$`\nu_{1}`$) and $`Q^{+}_{2}(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot)`$,
$`\overline{Q}_{2}(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot)`$ (parameterized by
$`\boldsymbol{\overline{\theta}_{2}},\boldsymbol{\theta^{+}_{2}}`$,
$`\mathbf{A_{2}}`$, induced policies $`\tilde{\mu}_{2}`$ and
$`\nu_{2}`$) respectively. Define

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{1}(s,\mu,\nu)\coloneqq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\mu(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{1}(s,i,j)\right]-\beta\text{KL}\left(\mu(\cdot|s)\|\mu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot|s)\right)+\beta\text{KL}\left(\nu(\cdot|s)\|\nu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot|s)\right),`$ |  |

then we have $`V_{1}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{1},\nu_{1})=V_{1}(s)`$, similarly
define $`V_{2}(s,\mu,\nu)`$, then we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\text{dist}(V_{1},V_{2})=\sup_{s}|V_{1}(s)-V_{2}(s)|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sup_{s}|V_{1}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{1},\nu_{1})-V_{2}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{2},\nu_{2})|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sup_{s}\max\left\{V_{1}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{1},\nu_{1})-V_{2}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{2},\nu_{2}),V_{2}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{2},\nu_{2})-V_{1}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{1},\nu_{1})\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sup_{s}\max\left\{V_{1}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{1},\nu_{1})-V_{2}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{1},\nu_{2}),V_{2}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{2},\nu_{2})-V_{1}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{2},\nu_{1})\right\}`$ |  | (136) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sup_{s}\max\left\{|V_{1}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{1},\nu_{1})-V_{2}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{1},\nu_{2})|,|V_{2}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{2},\nu_{2})-V_{1}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{2},\nu_{1})|\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sup_{s}\Bigg\{\underbrace{\left|\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{1}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{1}(s,i,j)\right]-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{2}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{2}(s,i,j)\right]\right|,\left|\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{2}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{1}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{1}(s,i,j)\right]-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{2}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{2}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{2}(s,i,j)\right]\right|}_{T_{Q}(s)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\beta\underbrace{\left|\text{KL}(\nu_{1}(\cdot|s)\|\nu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot|s))-\text{KL}(\nu_{2}(\cdot|s)\|\nu_{\text{ref}}(\cdot|s))\right|}_{T_{\text{KL}}(s)}\Bigg\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sup_{s}T_{Q}(s)+\sup_{s}T_{\text{KL}}(s).`$ |  | (137) |

Here, equation
([136](#A3.E136 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
follows from the fact that
$`V_{1}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{1},\nu_{1})\geq V_{1}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{2},\nu_{1})`$
and
$`V_{2}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{2},\nu_{2})\geq V_{2}(s,\tilde{\mu}_{1},\nu_{2})`$
from the definition of the best response. Now we bound $`T_{Q}(s)`$ as
follows. Consider the term

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{1}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{1}(s,i,j)\right]-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{2}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{2}(s,i,j)\right]\right|`$ |  | (138) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{1}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{1}(s,i,j)\right]-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{1}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{2}(s,i,j)\right]\right|+\left|\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{1}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{2}(s,i,j)\right]-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1}(\cdot|s)\\
j\sim\nu_{2}(\cdot|s)\end{subarray}}\left[Q^{+}_{2}(s,i,j)\right]\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sup_{i,j}|Q^{+}_{1}(s,i,j)-Q^{+}_{2}(s,i,j)|+\|\mathbb{E}_{i\sim\tilde{\mu}_{1}}[Q^{+}_{2}(s,i,\cdot)]\|_{\infty}\|\nu_{1}(\cdot|s)-\nu_{2}(\cdot|s)\|_{1}`$ |  | (139) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sup_{i,j}|Q^{+}_{1}(s,i,j)-Q^{+}_{2}(s,i,j)|+B_{2}^{+}\|\nu_{1}(\cdot|s)-\nu_{2}(\cdot|s)\|_{1}`$ |  | (140) |

Using the same argument (eq.
[138](#A3.E138 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")-[140](#A3.E140 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
for the other term in $`T_{Q}`$ we obtain the same bound and thus

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sup_{s}T_{Q}(s)\leq\sup_{s,i,j}|Q^{+}_{1}(s,i,j)-Q^{+}_{2}(s,i,j)|+\sup_{s}B_{2}^{+}\|\nu_{1}(\cdot|s)-\nu_{2}(\cdot|s)\|_{1}`$ |  | (141) |

Since $`\nu_{1}(\cdot|s)`$ and $`\nu_{2}(\cdot|s)`$ are the min-player
NE policies under the payoff matrices $`\overline{Q}_{1}`$ and
$`\overline{Q}_{2}`$ respectively and
$`\overline{Q}_{1},\overline{Q}_{2}\in[b_{2},B_{2}]`$, using lemma
[C.13](#A3.Thmlemma13 "Lemma C.13 (Stability under payoff perturbations). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
equations
([137](#A3.E137 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and
([141](#A3.E141 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\text{dist}(V_{1},V_{2})`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\sup_{s,i,j}|Q^{+}_{1}(s,i,j)-Q^{+}_{2}(s,i,j)|+2\sup_{s}B_{2}^{+}\sqrt{\frac{\sup_{i,j}|\overline{Q}_{1}(s,i,j)-\overline{Q}_{2}(s,i,j)|}{\beta}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+2\sup_{s}\left(\sup_{i,j}|\overline{Q}_{1}(s,i,j)-\overline{Q}_{2}(s,i,j)|+(B_{2}-b_{2})\sqrt{\frac{\sup_{i,j}|\overline{Q}_{1}(s,i,j)-\overline{Q}_{2}(s,i,j)|}{\beta}}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sup_{s,i,j}|Q^{+}_{1}(s,i,j)-Q^{+}_{2}(s,i,j)|+2\sup_{s,i,j}|\overline{Q}_{1}(s,i,j)-\overline{Q}_{2}(s,i,j)|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+2(B_{2}^{+}+B_{2}-b_{2})\sqrt{\frac{\sup_{s,i,j}|\overline{Q}_{1}(s,i,j)-\overline{Q}_{2}(s,i,j)|}{\beta}}`$ |  | (142) |

Thus to get an $`\varepsilon`$ cover we need an $`\varepsilon/3`$-cover
for $`\sup_{s,i,j}|Q^{+}_{1}(s,i,j)-Q^{+}_{2}(s,i,j)|`$
($`\mathcal{N}^{+}_{\varepsilon/3}`$) and a
$`\min\left\{\frac{\varepsilon}{6},\frac{\beta\varepsilon^{2}}{36(B_{2}^{+}+B_{2}-b_{2})^{2}}\right\}`$-cover
for $`\sup_{s,i,j}|\overline{Q}_{1}(s,i,j)-\overline{Q}_{2}(s,i,j)|`$
($`\overline{\mathcal{N}}_{\varepsilon/3}`$). Using the same steps as
the equations eqs
([133](#A3.E133 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))-([135](#A3.E135 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\log\mathcal{N}^{+}_{\varepsilon/3}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq d\log(1+12L^{+}/\varepsilon)+d^{2}\log\bigl(1+72d^{1/2}(B_{3})^{2}/(\lambda\varepsilon^{2})\bigr)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\log\overline{\mathcal{N}}_{\varepsilon/3}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{d\log(1+144L(B_{2}^{+}+B_{2}-b_{2})^{2}/\varepsilon^{2}\beta),\;d\log(1+24L/\varepsilon)\right\}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\leq d\log(1+144L(B_{2}^{+}+B_{2}-b_{2})^{2}/\varepsilon^{2}\beta)+d\log(1+24L/\varepsilon)`$ |  |

The last line follows since $`B_{3}=0`$ for the function class
$`\mathcal{\overline{Q}}`$ and thus we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\log\mathcal{N}_{\varepsilon}^{\texttt{sup}}\leq d\log(1+12L^{+}/\varepsilon)`$ | $`\displaystyle+d^{2}\log\bigl(1+72d^{1/2}(B_{3})^{2}/(\lambda\varepsilon^{2})\bigr)+d\log(1+24L/\varepsilon)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+d\log(1+144L(B_{2}^{+}+B_{2}-b_{2})^{2}/\varepsilon^{2}\beta)`$ |  |

 

###### Lemma C.12 (KL three-point identity).

Let $`\beta>0`$ and let $`\nu_{\text{ref}}\in\Delta_{n}`$ and
$`\mu_{\text{ref}}\in\Delta_{m}`$. Fix vectors $`c\in\mathbb{R}^{n}`$,
$`d\in\mathbb{R}^{m}`$ and define, for $`\mu\in\Delta_{m}`$,
$`\nu\in\Delta_{n}`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle F(\nu)\;`$ | $`\displaystyle:=\;\langle c,\nu\rangle+\beta\,\text{KL}(\nu\|\nu_{\text{ref}}),\qquad\text{and}\qquad G(\mu)\;:=\;\langle d,\mu\rangle-\beta\,\text{KL}(\mu\|\mu_{\text{ref}}).`$ |  |

Let $`\mu^{\star}`$ and $`\nu^{\star}`$ be the maximizer and minimizer
of $`G`$ and $`F`$ respectively. Then for all $`\mu\in\Delta_{m}`$,
$`\nu\in\Delta_{n}`$, such that
$`\text{supp}(\mu)\subseteq\text{supp}(\mu_{\text{ref}}),\;\text{supp}(\nu)\subseteq\text{supp}(\nu_{\text{ref}})`$
respectively, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle F(\nu)\;`$ | $`\displaystyle=\;F(\nu^{\star})\;+\;\beta\,\text{KL}(\nu\|\nu^{\star}),\qquad\text{and}\qquad G(\mu)\;=\;G(\mu^{\star})\;-\;\beta\,\text{KL}(\mu\|\mu^{\star}).`$ |  |

Proof. The maximizer $`\mu^{\star}\in\arg\max_{\mu\in\Delta_{m}}G(\mu)`$
is unique and satisfies

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mu^{\star}(i)\;=\;\frac{\mu_{\text{ref}}(i)\exp\!\big(d(i)/\beta\big)}{\sum_{k=1}^{m}\mu_{\text{ref}}(k)\exp\!\big(d(k)/\beta\big)}\qquad\forall i\in[m].
``` |  |

Define $`Z:=\sum_{k=1}^{m}\mu_{\text{ref}}(k)\exp(d(k)/\beta)`$, using
$`d(i)=\beta\log\frac{\mu^{\star}(i)}{\mu_{\text{ref}}(i)}+\beta\log Z`$
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle G(\mu)-G(\mu^{\star})=\;\langle d,\mu-\mu^{\star}\rangle-\beta\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big(\mu(i)\log\frac{\mu(i)}{\mu_{\text{ref}}(i)}-\mu^{\star}(i)\log\frac{\mu^{\star}(i)}{\mu_{\text{ref}}(i)}\Big).`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\beta\sum_{i=1}^{m}(\mu(i)-\mu^{\star}(i))\log\frac{\mu^{\star}(i)}{\mu_{\text{ref}}(i)}+\beta\log Z\sum_{i=1}^{m}(\mu(i)-\mu^{\star}(i))-\beta\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big(\mu(i)\log\frac{\mu(i)}{\mu_{\text{ref}}(i)}-\mu^{\star}(i)\log\frac{\mu^{\star}(i)}{\mu_{\text{ref}}(i)}\Big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\beta\sum_{i=1}^{m}(\mu(i)-\mu^{\star}(i))\log\frac{\mu^{\star}(i)}{\mu_{\text{ref}}(i)}-\beta\sum_{i=1}^{m}\Big(\mu(i)\log\frac{\mu(i)}{\mu_{\text{ref}}(i)}-\mu^{\star}(i)\log\frac{\mu^{\star}(i)}{\mu_{\text{ref}}(i)}\Big)`$ |  | (143) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=-\beta\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mu(i)\log\frac{\mu(i)}{\mu^{\star}(i)}\;=\;-\beta\,\text{KL}(\mu\|\mu^{\star}).`$ |  |  |

The $`\log Z`$ term in eq
([143](#A3.E143 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
vanishes since $`\sum_{i}\mu(i)=\sum_{i}\mu^{\star}(i)=1`$. Now
similarly the minimizer
$`\nu^{\star}\in\arg\min_{\nu\in\Delta_{n}}F(\nu)`$ is unique and
satisfies

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\nu^{\star}(j)\;=\;\frac{\nu_{\text{ref}}(j)\exp\!\big(-c(j)/\beta\big)}{\sum_{k=1}^{n}\nu_{\text{ref}}(k)\exp\!\big(-c(k)/\beta\big)}\qquad\forall j\in[n].
``` |  |

Define $`Z:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\nu_{\text{ref}}(k)\exp(-c(k)/\beta)`$, now
using
$`c(j)=-\beta\log\frac{\nu^{\star}(j)}{\nu_{\text{ref}}(j)}-\beta\log Z.`$

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle F(\nu)-F(\nu^{\star})=\;\langle c,\nu-\nu^{\star}\rangle+\beta\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Big(\nu(j)\log\frac{\nu(j)}{\nu_{\text{ref}}(j)}-\nu^{\star}(j)\log\frac{\nu^{\star}(j)}{\nu_{\text{ref}}(j)}\Big).`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=-\beta\sum_{j=1}^{n}(\nu(j)-\nu^{\star}(j))\log\frac{\nu^{\star}(j)}{\nu_{\text{ref}}(j)}-\beta\log Z\sum_{j=1}^{n}(\nu(j)-\nu^{\star}(j))+\beta\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Big(\nu(j)\log\frac{\nu(j)}{\nu_{\text{ref}}(j)}-\nu^{\star}(j)\log\frac{\nu^{\star}(j)}{\nu_{\text{ref}}(j)}\Big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=-\beta\sum_{j=1}^{n}(\nu(j)-\nu^{\star}(j))\log\frac{\nu^{\star}(j)}{\nu_{\text{ref}}(j)}+\beta\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Big(\nu(j)\log\frac{\nu(j)}{\nu_{\text{ref}}(j)}-\nu^{\star}(j)\log\frac{\nu^{\star}(j)}{\nu_{\text{ref}}(j)}\Big)`$ |  | (144) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\beta\sum_{j=1}^{n}\nu(j)\log\frac{\nu(j)}{\nu^{\star}(j)}\;=\;\beta\,\text{KL}(\nu\|\nu^{\star}).`$ |  |  |

The $`\log Z`$ term in
eq. ([144](#A3.E144 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
vanishes since $`\sum_{j}\nu(j)=\sum_{j}\nu^{\star}(j)=1`$.  

###### Lemma C.13 (Stability under payoff perturbations).

Let $`A\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}`$ and
$`A^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}`$ be two payoff matrices with

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
A-A^{\prime}=\delta,\qquad\|\delta\|_{\max}:=\sup_{i\in[m],\,j\in[n]}|\delta(i,j)|=\sup_{i,j}\big|A(i,j)-A^{\prime}(i,j)\big|.
``` |  |

The reference policies $`\mu_{\text{ref}}\in\Delta_{m}`$ and
$`\nu_{\text{ref}}\in\Delta_{n}`$ have full support and fix $`\beta>0`$.
Let $`(\mu,\nu)`$ and $`(\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})`$ denote the unique
Nash equilibrium policies of the KL-regularized zero-sum matrix games
induced by $`A`$ and $`A^{\prime}`$, respectively, with reference
policies $`(\mu_{\text{ref}},\nu_{\text{ref}})`$ and regularization
strength $`\beta`$ for both players. If all the entries of $`A`$ and
$`A^{\prime}`$ lie in $`[A_{\min},A_{\max}]`$. Then the following
statements hold

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big|\text{KL}(\nu\|\nu_{\text{ref}})-\text{KL}(\nu^{\prime}\|\nu_{\text{ref}})\big|\;`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\;\frac{2}{\beta}\,\|\delta\|_{\max}\;+\;\frac{2\,(A_{\max}-A_{\min})}{\beta}\,\sqrt{\frac{\|\delta\|_{\max}}{\beta}},`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\|\nu-\nu^{\prime}\|_{1}\;`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\;2\sqrt{\frac{\|\delta\|_{\max}}{\beta}}.`$ |  | (145) |

Proof. Define, for any payoff matrix
$`\tilde{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}`$, and policies
$`\tilde{\mu},\tilde{\nu}`$ which have the same support as
$`\mu_{\text{ref}},\nu_{\text{ref}}`$

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
f_{\tilde{A}}(\tilde{\mu},\tilde{\nu})\;:=\;\tilde{\mu}^{\top}\tilde{A}\tilde{\nu}\;-\;\beta\,\text{KL}(\tilde{\mu}\|\mu_{\text{ref}})\;+\;\beta\,\text{KL}(\tilde{\nu}\|\nu_{\text{ref}}),\qquad\mu\in\Delta_{m},\ \nu\in\Delta_{n}.
``` |  |

Let $`(\mu,\nu)`$ and $`(\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})`$ denote the KL
regularized Nash equilibrium policies for $`A`$ and $`A^{\prime}`$,
respectively, and recall that $`\delta=A-A^{\prime}`$. By
Lemma [C.12](#A3.Thmlemma12 "Lemma C.12 (KL three-point identity). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
(minimization form), since
$`\nu\in\arg\min_{\tilde{\nu}\in\Delta_{n}}f_{A}(\mu,\tilde{\nu})`$, we
have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
f_{A}(\mu,\nu^{\prime})\;=\;f_{A}(\mu,\nu)\;+\;\beta\,\text{KL}(\nu^{\prime}\|\nu),
``` |  | (146) |

using
Lemma [C.12](#A3.Thmlemma12 "Lemma C.12 (KL three-point identity). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
(maximization form), since
$`\mu\in\arg\max_{\tilde{\mu}\in\Delta_{m}}f_{A}(\tilde{\mu},\nu)`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
f_{A}(\mu^{\prime},\nu)\;=\;f_{A}(\mu,\nu)\;-\;\beta\,\text{KL}(\mu^{\prime}\|\mu).
``` |  | (147) |

Subtracting
([147](#A3.E147 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
from
([146](#A3.E146 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
yields

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
f_{A}(\mu,\nu^{\prime})-f_{A}(\mu^{\prime},\nu)\;=\;\beta\Big(\text{KL}(\nu^{\prime}\|\nu)+\text{KL}(\mu^{\prime}\|\mu)\Big).
``` |  | (148) |

Similarly, under $`A^{\prime}`$ we have
$`\nu^{\prime}\in\arg\min_{\tilde{\nu}\in\Delta_{n}}f_{A^{\prime}}(\mu^{\prime},\tilde{\nu})`$
and
$`\mu^{\prime}\in\arg\max_{\tilde{\mu}\in\Delta_{m}}f_{A^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mu},\nu^{\prime})`$,
hence

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle f_{A^{\prime}}(\mu^{\prime},\nu)\;`$ | $`\displaystyle=\;f_{A^{\prime}}(\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})\;+\;\beta\,\text{KL}(\nu\|\nu^{\prime}),`$ |  | (149) |
|  | $`\displaystyle f_{A^{\prime}}(\mu,\nu^{\prime})\;`$ | $`\displaystyle=\;f_{A^{\prime}}(\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})\;-\;\beta\,\text{KL}(\mu\|\mu^{\prime}).`$ |  | (150) |

Subtracting
([150](#A3.E150 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
from
([149](#A3.E149 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
yields

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
f_{A^{\prime}}(\mu^{\prime},\nu)-f_{A^{\prime}}(\mu,\nu^{\prime})\;=\;\beta\Big(\text{KL}(\nu\|\nu^{\prime})+\text{KL}(\mu\|\mu^{\prime})\Big).
``` |  | (151) |

Adding
([148](#A3.E148 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and
([151](#A3.E151 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
gives

|  |  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\beta\Big(\text{KL}(\nu^{\prime}\|\nu)+\text{KL}(\nu\|\nu^{\prime})+\text{KL}(\mu^{\prime}\|\mu)+\text{KL}(\mu\|\mu^{\prime})\Big)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\Big(f_{A}(\mu,\nu^{\prime})-f_{A}(\mu^{\prime},\nu)\Big)+\Big(f_{A^{\prime}}(\mu^{\prime},\nu)-f_{A^{\prime}}(\mu,\nu^{\prime})\Big).`$ |  | (152) |

Using $`A^{\prime}=A-\delta`$, we have
$`f_{A^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mu},\tilde{\nu})\;=\;f_{A}(\tilde{\mu},\tilde{\nu})-\tilde{\mu}^{\top}\delta\,\tilde{\nu}.`$
for any $`(\tilde{\mu},\tilde{\nu})`$. Substituting this into the RHS of
eq
[152](#A3.E152 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
yields the exact identity

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\beta\Big(\text{KL}(\nu^{\prime}\|\nu)+\text{KL}(\nu\|\nu^{\prime})+\text{KL}(\mu^{\prime}\|\mu)+\text{KL}(\mu\|\mu^{\prime})\Big)\;=\;-\mu^{\prime\top}\delta\,\nu\;+\;\mu^{\top}\delta\,\nu^{\prime}.
``` |  | (153) |

By eq:
([153](#A3.E153 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and nonnegativity of KL, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\text{KL}(\nu\|\nu^{\prime})\;\leq\;\frac{1}{\beta}\left(\mu^{\prime\top}\delta\,\nu\;-\;\mu^{\top}\delta\,\nu^{\prime}\right)\;\leq\;\frac{1}{\beta}\left(\big|\mu^{\prime\top}\delta\,\nu\big|+\big|\mu^{\top}\delta\,\nu^{\prime}\big|\right)\leq\;\frac{2}{\beta}\|\delta\|_{\max}.
``` |  | (154) |

Note that the same bound also applies to
$`\text{KL}(\nu^{\prime}\|\nu)`$. Now by Pinsker’s inequality and
([154](#A3.E154 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
we have the second part of the lemma

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\|\nu-\nu^{\prime}\|_{1}\;\leq\;\sqrt{2\,\text{KL}(\nu\|\nu^{\prime})}\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{\|\delta\|_{\max}}{\beta}}.`$ |  | (155) |

For any $`p,q,r`$ with the same support

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\text{KL}(p\|r)-\text{KL}(q\|r)\;=\;\text{KL}(p\|q)\;+\;\big\langle p-q,\log(q/r)\big\rangle.
``` |  |

Applying this with $`(p,q,r)=(\nu,\nu^{\prime},\nu_{\text{ref}})`$ and
taking absolute values gives

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big|\text{KL}(\nu\|\nu_{\text{ref}})-\text{KL}(\nu^{\prime}\|\nu_{\text{ref}})\big|`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\text{KL}(\nu\|\nu^{\prime})\;+\;\|\nu-\nu^{\prime}\|_{1}\cdot\left\|\log\!\frac{\nu^{\prime}}{\nu_{\text{ref}}}\right\|_{\infty}.`$ |  | (156) |

Since
$`\nu^{\prime}\in\arg\min_{\nu^{\prime}\in\Delta_{n}}f_{A^{\prime}}(\mu^{\prime},\nu^{\prime})`$,
it is the KL-regularized best response to $`\mu^{\prime}`$ under
$`A^{\prime}`$, hence

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\nu^{\prime}(j)\;=\;\frac{\nu_{\text{ref}}(j)\exp\!\big(-(A^{\prime\top}\mu^{\prime})(j)/\beta\big)}{\sum_{k=1}^{n}\nu_{\text{ref}}(k)\exp\!\big(-(A^{\prime\top}\mu^{\prime})(k)/\beta\big)}\qquad\forall j\in[n].
``` |  | (157) |

Let
$`Z:=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\nu_{\text{ref}}(k)\exp\big(-(A^{\prime\top}\mu^{\prime})(k)/\beta\big)`$.
Then

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\log\frac{\nu^{\prime}(j)}{\nu_{\text{ref}}(j)}\;=\;-\frac{(A^{\prime\top}\mu^{\prime})(j)}{\beta}-\log Z.
``` |  | (158) |

Now for every $`j\in[n]`$,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
(A^{\prime\top}\mu^{\prime})(j)\;=\;\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mu^{\prime}(i)A^{\prime}(i,j)\in[A_{\min},A_{\max}],\qquad\text{and}\qquad Z\in\Big[e^{-A_{\max}/\beta},\,e^{-A_{\min}/\beta}\Big]
``` |  |

which implies
$`\log Z\in\Big[-\frac{A_{\max}}{\beta},-\frac{A_{\min}}{\beta}\Big]`$
and from
([158](#A3.E158 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have, for all $`j`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\log\frac{\nu^{\prime}(j)}{\nu_{\text{ref}}(j)}\right|\leq\frac{A_{\max}-A_{\min}}{\beta},\qquad\text{hence}\qquad\left\|\log\!\frac{\nu^{\prime}}{\nu_{\text{ref}}}\right\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{A_{\max}-A_{\min}}{\beta}.`$ |  | (159) |

Combining equations
([154](#A3.E154 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),([155](#A3.E155 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
([156](#A3.E156 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
([159](#A3.E159 "In C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\big|\text{KL}(\nu\|\nu_{\text{ref}})-\text{KL}(\nu^{\prime}\|\nu_{\text{ref}})\big|\leq\frac{2}{\beta}\|\delta\|_{\max}+2\sqrt{\frac{\|\delta\|_{\max}}{\beta}}\cdot\frac{A_{\max}-A_{\min}}{\beta},
``` |  |

 

## Appendix D Additional Discussions

### D.1 Reduction to single-agent settings

Both $`\mathrm{OMG}`$ and $`\mathrm{SOMG}`$ can be used in the
single-agent setting for bandits and RL respectively by setting the
action set (and hence even the reference policy) of the min player to a
singleton. For matrix games, this results in the same bound as Theorem
[2.1](#S2.Thmtheorem1 "Theorem 2.1. ‣ Regret Guarantees. ‣ 2.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 2 Two-Player Zero-Sum Matrix Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").

However, in the RL setting we can obtain a tighter dependence on $`H`$.
Using a smaller bonus term and a projection operator with linear
dependence on $`H`$, we achieve improved regret guarantees. When
specialized to the single-agent RL setting, this gives a regret bound of
$`\min\!\left\{\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\!\left(d^{3/2}H^{2}\sqrt{T}\right),\,\mathcal{O}\!\left(\beta^{-1}d^{3}H^{5}\log^{2}(T/\delta)\right)\right\}`$.
The value function in the game-theoretic setting is given by

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
V_{h}^{\mu,\nu}(s):=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k=h}^{H}r_{k}(s_{k},i,j)-\beta\text{KL}(\mu_{k}(\cdot|s_{k})\|\mu_{\text{ref},k}(\cdot|s_{k}))+\beta\text{KL}(\nu_{k}(\cdot|s_{k})\|\nu_{\text{ref},k}(\cdot|s_{k}))\middle|s_{h}=s\right],
``` |  |

This design of bonus terms and projection operators is possible due to
the fact that when the min player action set is restricted to a
singleton, the positive KL terms disappear and the value function (and
hence the $`Q`$ functions) will now be bounded between $`(-\infty,H]`$
instead of $`(-\infty,\infty)`$. Specifically, for a policy $`\pi`$, the
value function in KL-regularized RL is given by

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
V_{h}^{\pi}(s):=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k=h}^{H}r_{k}(s_{k},i,j)-\beta\text{KL}(\pi_{k}(\cdot|s_{k})\|\pi_{\text{ref},k}(\cdot|s_{k}))\middle|s_{h}=s\right],
``` |  |

thus the projection for best-response $`Q`$-function can now use
$`\mathcal{O}(H)`$ ceiling in
Eq. ([21b](#S3.E21.2 "In 21 ‣ 1st item ‣ 3.2 Algorithm Development ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")).
We do not need $`Q^{-}`$
(([16c](#S3.E16.3 "In 16 ‣ 5 ‣ Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),([17c](#S3.E17.3 "In 17 ‣ 6 ‣ Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")))
in $`\mathrm{SOMG}`$ since the min player makes no decisions (action set
is singleton). As a result of this, we get an $`H`$-dependence in the
bonus term and hence a

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\min\!\left\{\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\!\left(d^{3/2}H^{2}\sqrt{T}\right),\,\mathcal{\widetilde{O}}\!\left(\beta^{-1}d^{3}H^{5}\log^{2}(T/\delta)\right)\right\}
``` |  |

regret. This matches the best known regret bound obtained by [Zhao et
al. \[2025b\]](#bib.bib1)⁵⁵ 5 The bound is adopted for linear MDP where
the log covering number $`\log(\mathcal{N})`$ grows as $`d^{2}\log(T)`$
(lemma
[C.10](#A3.Thmlemma10 "Lemma C.10 (Covering number of the MSE Value function class in Algorithm ). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")),
we use $`\sum_{h=1}^{H}r_{h}\in[0,H]`$ while [Zhao et al.
\[2025b\]](#bib.bib1) use $`\sum_{h=1}^{H}r_{h}\in[0,1]`$, translating
this to our setting gives an additional $`H^{2}`$ factor due to
dependency on the square of the bonus term.
$`d(\mathcal{F},\lambda,T)=\sum_{h=1}^{H}d(\mathcal{F}_{h},\lambda,T)`$
scales at $`dH`$ in single-agent KL-regularized RL.

### D.2 Extension to general function approximation

$`\mathrm{SOMG}`$ can be extended beyond the linear MDPs to RKHS/General
function approximation for the $`Q`$ function class with local
(state-action wise) optimism using standard arguments from the
literature. For example, to extend $`\mathrm{SOMG}`$ to general function
approximation, we additionally need a standard realizability assumption
on the value functions class induced by $`\mathrm{SOMG}`$ in equation
[20](#S3.EGx17 "In 9 ‣ Algorithm 2 ‣ 3.1 Problem Setup ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
which we get for free in Linear MDP (From Assumption
[3](#Thmassumption3 "Assumption 3 (Linear MDP ( ) ). ‣ 3.3 Theoretical Guarantees ‣ 3 Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and lemma
[C.8](#A3.Thmlemma8 "Lemma C.8 (Linearity of the Q function). ‣ C.5 Auxiliary Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and a bounded log covering number assumption \[[Zhao et al.,
2025b](#bib.bib1), [Ye et al., 2023](#bib.bib2)\].

Beyond this, the only parts of the $`\mathrm{SOMG}`$ proof that are
specific to linear MDP are lemmas
[C.1](#A3.Thmlemma1 "Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"),
[C.2](#A3.Thmlemma2 "Lemma C.2 (Concentration of Superoptimistic Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
which define bonuses $`b_{h,t}^{\texttt{mse}}`$, $`b_{h,t}`$,
respectively, and the bounding of the sum of squares of bonuses in
equations
([93](#A3.E93 "In Step 3: Finishing up. ‣ C.2 Proof of Theorem : Logarithmic Regret Bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
and
([107](#A3.E107 "In Step 3: Finishing up. ‣ C.3.1 𝛽>0: Regularized setting ‣ C.3 Proof of Theorem : Traditional √𝑇 bound ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games"))
using the elliptical potential lemma
[A.6](#A1.Thmlemma6 "Lemma A.6 (Elliptical Potential Lemma). ‣ Appendix A Useful Lemmas ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games").
Replacing these components with bonuses used with general function
approximation, as done in [Zhao et al. \[2025b\]](#bib.bib1), [Zhao et
al. \[2025c\]](#bib.bib37), extends our results to general function
approximation settings.

Specifically, the width of the uncertainty set at step $`h`$, time $`t`$
for $`(s,i,j)`$ in linear function approximation is specified using the
covariance matrix
$`\mathcal{U}_{h,t}^{\text{lin}}(s,i,j;\mathcal{D}_{t-1}):=\|\phi(s,i,j)\|_{\Sigma_{h,t}^{-1}}`$
and the bonus is of the form
$`\eta\cdot\min\left\{1,\mathcal{U}_{h,t}^{\text{lin}}(s,i,j;\mathcal{D}_{t-1})\right\}`$
which is equal to
$`\eta\cdot\mathcal{U}_{h,t}^{\text{lin}}(s,i,j;\mathcal{D}_{t-1})`$
when regularization $`\lambda>1`$ (both $`b_{h,t}^{\texttt{mse}}`$ and
$`b_{h,t}`$ take this form) with $`\eta`$ being a constant that depends
on problem parameters. Under general function approximation (with a
function class $`\mathcal{F}`$) the width of the uncertainty set is
given by \[[Agarwal et al., 2023](#bib.bib75), [Ye et al.,
2023](#bib.bib2), [Zhao et al., 2025b](#bib.bib1)\]

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathcal{U}_{h,t}^{\text{gen}}(s,i,j;\mathcal{D}_{t-1}):=\sup_{f,f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}}\frac{|f(s,i,j)-f^{\prime}(s,i,j)|}{\sqrt{\lambda+\sum\limits_{s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}\in\mathcal{D}_{t-1}}(f(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h})-f^{\prime}(s_{h},i_{h},j_{h}))^{2}}},
``` |  |

where $`\lambda`$ is the regularization parameter. To obtain bounds for
general function approximation we use $`\mathcal{U}_{h,t}^{\text{gen}}`$
instead of $`\mathcal{U}_{h,t}^{\text{lin}}`$ to create confidence
intervals in lemmas
[C.1](#A3.Thmlemma1 "Lemma C.1 (Concentration of MSE Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and
[C.2](#A3.Thmlemma2 "Lemma C.2 (Concentration of Superoptimistic Bellman errors). ‣ C.1 Supporting Lemmas ‣ Appendix C Markov Game Proofs ‣ Achieving Logarithmic Regret in KL-Regularized Zero-Sum Markov Games")
and use the Eluder dimension \[[Agarwal et al., 2023](#bib.bib75), [Zhao
et al., 2025b](#bib.bib1)\]

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
d(\mathcal{F},T):=\sup_{s_{1:T},\,i_{1:T},\,j_{1:T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\min\bigl(1,\,[\mathcal{U}_{h,t}^{\text{gen}}(s_{t},i_{t},j_{t};\mathcal{D}_{t-1})]^{2}\bigr),
``` |  |

instead of elliptical potential lemma to bound the sum of squares of
bonus terms. Similar arguments extend $`\mathrm{OMG}`$ to general
function approximation.
````
